# Adjacent Swarm owner, correspondence, drive, final-output, fault, and test source bundle (correction 5)

Exact source SHA: 95323ff17cb29928e31467f651ef03bae2099c14

Exact source tree: 43b47bbd49a6053d270b3e15cc141cb1b1bb86da

This bundle preserves the exact repository-wide 24-path lexical hit set as a regression gate and then binds the complete semantic supplements omitted by correction 4. It exposes the existing one-occurrence output seal, the complete final correspondence join and ingress wrapper, drive-to-final-output carriers and typed fault algebras, both direct-run consumers, the ProviderHostExecutionSession fields/mint/reexport/selected branch/Drop, its durable-loader caller and dependency direction, capability-model mismatch/custody-preservation tests, and static-host nominal accepted/rejected/authored correspondence tests. No static-host mismatch or replay test exists at the bound SHA; new static-host mismatch and retained-libbun replay tests remain mandatory implementation output. A new package/invocation brand must extend or atomically replace the existing seal; a parallel seal is forbidden.

Every compact excerpt names the complete owning item span selected from the exact file, plus the full-file blob/SHA-256/byte identity and an excerpt SHA-256. Small bounded files are included completely. The repository-wide discovery gate runs before this fixed closure is rendered.

    63  impl SsExternalCapabilityProviderHost {
    64      pub fn new_for_source_compiler_owner_v1(
    65          working_directory: &Path,
    66      ) -> SsExternalCapabilityProviderResult<Self> {

    70      pub fn new_for_ss_test_runtime_provider_owner_v1(

    72      ) -> SsExternalCapabilityProviderResult<Self> {
    73          Self::new_with_dropped_process_output(working_directory)
    74      }
    75
    76      fn new_with_dropped_process_output(
    77          working_directory: &Path,
    78      ) -> SsExternalCapabilityProviderResult<Self> {
    79          let mut config = libbun::BunRuntimeConfig::new("libbun", working_directory);
    80          config.stdout = libbun::SinkPolicy::Drop;
    81          config.stderr = libbun::SinkPolicy::Drop;
    82          config.log = libbun::SinkPolicy::Drop;
    83          let backend = LibbunExternalCapabilityProviderBackend::open(config)?;
    84          Ok(Self {
    85              working_directory: working_directory.to_path_buf(),
    86              backend,
    87          })
    88      }
    89
    90      pub fn shutdown(&mut self) -> SsExternalCapabilityProviderResult<()> {
    91          self.backend.shutdown()?;
    92          Ok(())
    93      }
    94  }
    95
    96  pub fn install_libbun_external_capability_provider_for_ss_runtime_owner_v1(
    97      provider_host_set: SsRuntimeProviderHostSet,
    98      working_directory: &Path,
    99  ) -> SsExternalCapabilityProviderResult<SsRuntimeProviderHostSet> {
   100      let factory = Arc::new(SsLibbunExternalCapabilityProviderFactory {
   101          working_directory: working_directory.to_path_buf(),
   102      });
   103      let host = ExternalTransportCapabilityProviderHost::libbun_for_ss_external_capability_provider_owner_v1(
   104          factory,
   105      )?;
   106      provider_host_set
   107          .with_external_transport_capability_provider_for_ss_external_provider_owner_v1(host)
   108          .map_err(Into::into)
   109  }

   121  impl DurableExternalCapabilityProvider for SsExternalCapabilityProviderHost {
   122      fn invoke_manifest_resolved_call_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(
   123          &mut self,
   124          invocation: DurableExternalProviderInvocationAuthority,
   125      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, CapabilitySdkError>
   126      {
   127          let (call_authority, provider_input, output_settlement) = invocation
   128              .into_call_input_and_output_settlement_for_durable_external_provider_owner_v1();
   129          let (contract, provider_module_import_path, provider_module_export) =
   130              call_authority.into_contract_and_module_for_durable_external_provider_owner_v1();
   131          if !matches!(&provider_input, ProviderValue::Array(_)) {
   132              return Err(CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(
   133                  "manifest-resolved external provider call must carry its sealed positional argument vector"
   134                      .to_owned(),
   135              ));
   136          }
   137          let input_json = provider_value_to_canonical_json_v1(&provider_input).map_err(|error| {
   138              CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(format!(
   139                  "manifest-resolved external provider input canonicalization failed: {error}"
   140              ))
   141          })?;
   142          let input = serde_json::from_str(&input_json).map_err(|error| {
   143              CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(format!(
   144                  "manifest-resolved external provider input JSON decode failed: {error}"
   145              ))
   146          })?;
   147          let fingerprint = contract
   148              .fingerprint()
   149              .ok_or_else(|| {
   150                  CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(
   151                      "manifest-resolved external provider contract requires an exact fingerprint"
   152                          .to_owned(),
   153                  )
   154              })?
   155              .as_str()
   156              .to_owned();
   157          let invocation_id = format!(
   158              "ss-libbun-external-provider-{}",
   159              NEXT_LIBBUN_INVOCATION_ID.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
   160          );
   161          let provider_adapter_source = libbun_provider_adapter_source_for_selected_route(
   162              &provider_module_import_path,
   163              &provider_module_export,
   164              contract.package_specifier(),
   165              contract.export_name(),
   166          )?;
   167          let request = libbun::ProviderRequest {
   168              contract: libbun::ProviderContractIdentity {
   169                  package: contract.package_specifier().to_owned(),
   170                  capability: contract.export_name().to_owned(),
   171                  contract_fingerprint: fingerprint,
   172              },
   173              domain: libbun::ProviderDomainClass::JavaScriptExternalTransport,
   174              module: libbun::BunModuleSpec::Source {
   175                  module_id: format!("{invocation_id}-adapter"),
   176                  source: provider_adapter_source,
   177              },
   178              export: LIBBUN_PROVIDER_ADAPTER_EXPORT.to_owned(),
   179              input: libbun::StructuralValue(input),
   180          };
   181          let descriptor = libbun::ProviderInvocationDescriptor::new(invocation_id.clone())
   182              .with_output_policy(libbun::InvocationOutputPolicy::Drop);
   183          let finished = self
   184              .backend
   185              .begin_invocation(descriptor)
   186              .and_then(|lease| {
   187                  lease.settle_provider(
   188                      request,
   189                      libbun::ProviderSettleOptions::new(libbun::ProviderDeadline::after(
   190                          Duration::from_secs(30),
   191                      ))
   192                      .with_call_id(invocation_id),
   193                  )
   194              })
   195              .and_then(|settled| settled.finish())
   196              .map_err(external_provider_fault)?;
   197          let output = match finished.receipt {
   198              libbun::SettledProviderReceipt::Ready {
   199                  result: libbun::ProviderCallResult::Ok(output),
   200                  ..
   201              } => output.0,
   202              libbun::SettledProviderReceipt::Ready {
   203                  result: libbun::ProviderCallResult::Err(error),
   204                  ..
   205              } => {
   206                  return Err(CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(
   207                      format!(
   208                          "manifest-resolved external provider rejected the call ({}): {}",
   209                          error.code, error.message
   210                      ),
   211                  ));
   212              }
   213              libbun::SettledProviderReceipt::Failed(failure) => {
   214                  return Err(CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(
   215                      format!("manifest-resolved external provider execution failed: {failure:?}"),
   216                  ));
   217              }
   218          };
   219          let output_json = serde_json::to_string(&output).map_err(|error| {
   220              CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(format!(
   221                  "manifest-resolved external provider output JSON encode failed: {error}"
   222              ))
   223          })?;
   224          let output = provider_value_from_canonical_json_v1(&output_json).map_err(|error| {
   225              CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(format!(
   226                  "manifest-resolved external provider output admission failed: {error}"
   227              ))
   228          })?;
   229          output_settlement.settle_ready_for_durable_external_provider_owner_v1(output)
   230      }

   237  /// Builds the transport-only adapter for one already-selected external route.
   238  ///
   239  /// `libbun` invokes one module export with one structural input. Swarm provider
   240  /// modules instead expose a zero-argument, branded factory whose result is the
   241  /// callable behavior. The adapter preserves those distinct stages: Rust embeds
   242  /// the upstream-selected module path and export, the factory receives no host
   243  /// input, and only the resolved behavior receives the sealed positional vector.
   244  fn libbun_provider_adapter_source_for_selected_route(
   245      provider_module_import_path: &str,
   246      provider_module_export: &str,
   247      contract_package_specifier: &str,
   248      contract_export_name: &str,
   249  ) -> Result<String, CapabilitySdkError> {
   250      let provider_module_url =
   251          file_url_for_selected_provider_path(Path::new(provider_module_import_path))?;
   252      let provider_module_url = adapter_string_literal("provider module URL", &provider_module_url)?;
   253      let provider_module_export =
   254          adapter_string_literal("provider module export", provider_module_export)?;
   255      let contract_package_specifier =
   256          adapter_string_literal("contract package specifier", contract_package_specifier)?;
   257      let contract_export_name =
   258          adapter_string_literal("contract export name", contract_export_name)?;
   259
   260      Ok(format!(
   261          r#"const providerModuleImportPath = {provider_module_url};
   262  const providerModuleExport = {provider_module_export};
   263  const contractPackageSpecifier = {contract_package_specifier};
   264  const contractExportName = {contract_export_name};
   265  const providerFactoryKind = "swarm.provider.factory.v1";
   266
   267  function requireProviderFactory(value) {{
   268    if (typeof value !== "function" || value.kind !== providerFactoryKind) {{
   269      throw new TypeError(
   270        `capability '${{contractPackageSpecifier}}:${{contractExportName}}' must resolve to the selected @swarm/provider factory`,
   271      );
   272    }}
   273    return value;
   274  }}
   275
   276  function requireCallableProvider(value) {{
   277    if (typeof value !== "function") {{
   278      throw new TypeError(
   279        `provider '${{contractPackageSpecifier}}:${{contractExportName}}' did not resolve to a callable behavior`,
   280      );
   281    }}
   282    return value;
   283  }}
   284
   285  function requireSealedPositionalArguments(value) {{
   286    if (!Array.isArray(value)) {{
   287      throw new TypeError(
   288        `provider '${{contractPackageSpecifier}}:${{contractExportName}}' requires a sealed positional argument vector`,
   289      );
   290    }}
   291    return value;
   292  }}
   293
   294  export async function {LIBBUN_PROVIDER_ADAPTER_EXPORT}(positionalArguments) {{
   295    const providerModule = await import(providerModuleImportPath);
   296    const factory = requireProviderFactory(providerModule?.[providerModuleExport]);
   297    const provider = requireCallableProvider(await factory());
   298    return await provider(...requireSealedPositionalArguments(positionalArguments));
   299  }}
   300  "#
   301      ))
   302  }
   303
   304  fn adapter_string_literal(field: &'static str, value: &str) -> Result<String, CapabilitySdkError> {
   305      serde_json::to_string(value).map_err(|error| {
   306          CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(format!(
   307              "manifest-resolved external provider adapter {field} projection failed: {error}"
   308          ))
   309      })
   310  }
   311
   312  fn file_url_for_selected_provider_path(path: &Path) -> Result<String, CapabilitySdkError> {
   313      let path = path.to_str().ok_or_else(|| {
   314          CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(format!(
   315              "manifest-resolved external provider module path is not valid UTF-8: {}",
   316              path.display()
   317          ))
   318      })?;
   319      let mut url = String::from("file://");
   320      for byte in path.as_bytes() {
   321          match byte {
   322              b'A'..=b'Z' | b'a'..=b'z' | b'0'..=b'9' | b'-' | b'.' | b'_' | b'~' | b'/' => {
   323                  url.push(char::from(*byte));
   324              }
   325              _ => url.push_str(&format!("%{byte:02X}")),
   326          }
   327      }
   328      Ok(url)
   329  }
   330
   331  fn external_provider_fault(error: impl ToString) -> CapabilitySdkError {
   332      CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(format!(
   333          "retained libbun external provider failed: {}",
   334          error.to_string()
   335      ))
   336  }
   337
   338  #[cfg(test)]
   339  mod tests {
   340      use super::*;
   341
   342      #[test]
   343      fn selected_route_adapter_separates_factory_resolution_from_positional_invocation() {
   344          let source = libbun_provider_adapter_source_for_selected_route(
   345              "/tmp/provider path/swarm.ts",
   346              "create",
   347              "@fixture/resource-contract",
   348              "create",
   349          )
   350          .expect("selected provider adapter source");
   351
   352          assert!(source.contains(
   353              r#"const providerModuleImportPath = "file:///tmp/provider%20path/swarm.ts";"#
   354          ));
   355          assert!(source.contains(r#"const providerModuleExport = "create";"#));
   356          assert!(source.contains("providerModule?.[providerModuleExport]"));
   357          assert!(source.contains("value.kind !== providerFactoryKind"));
   358          assert!(source.contains("await factory()"));
   359          assert!(!source.contains("factory(positionalArguments)"));
   360          assert!(
   361              source.contains("provider(...requireSealedPositionalArguments(positionalArguments))")
   362          );
   363      }
   364  }

   225      pub(in crate::direct_run) fn execute_selected_product_session_provider_effect_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   226          root_scope_id: &str,
   227          session_id: &str,
   228          node_id: &str,
   229          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
   230          run_occurred_at_clock: &EventAppendOccurredAtClock,
   231      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, String> {
   232          let Some(operation) =
   233              swarm_event_product_session_operation_for_provider_id(request.provider_id())
   234          else {
   235              return Err(json!({
   236                  "kind": "direct_run_product_session_event_operation_admission_drift",
   237                  "reason": "the product-session boundary predicate admitted this provider to the kernel-owned event route, but the capability SDK catalogue names no finite product-session operation for it",
   238                  "provider_id": request.provider_id(),
   239              })
   240              .to_string());
   241          };
   242          match operation {
   243              SwarmEventProductSessionOperation::PublishEvent => {
   244                  Self::execute_rust_sdk_publish_event_provider_effect(
   245                      root_scope_id,
   246                      session_id,
   247                      node_id,
   248                      request,
   249                      run_occurred_at_clock,
   250                  )
   251              }
   252              SwarmEventProductSessionOperation::SsTestEventCaptureHeadRead => {
   253                  Self::execute_ss_test_event_capture_provider_effect(root_scope_id, request)
   254              }
   255              SwarmEventProductSessionOperation::SsTestEventCaptureRangeRead => {
   256                  Self::execute_ss_test_event_read_provider_effect(root_scope_id, request)
   257              }
   258              SwarmEventProductSessionOperation::MintObservationGrant => {
   259                  Self::execute_swarm_event_mint_observation_grant_provider_effect(request)
   260              }
   261              SwarmEventProductSessionOperation::ReadObservationStream => {
   262                  Self::execute_ss_test_event_read_observation_stream_provider_effect(request)
   263              }
   264              SwarmEventProductSessionOperation::ControlObservationStream => {
   265                  Self::execute_ss_test_event_control_observation_stream_provider_effect(request)
   266              }
   267              SwarmEventProductSessionOperation::ReadObservationSourceRegistry => {
   268                  Self::execute_ss_test_event_read_observation_source_registry_provider_effect(request)
   269              }
   270              SwarmEventProductSessionOperation::ReadObservationCursor => {
   271                  Self::execute_ss_test_event_read_observation_cursor_provider_effect(request)
   272              }
   273              SwarmEventProductSessionOperation::OpenObservationStream => {
   274                  Self::execute_ss_test_event_open_observation_stream_provider_effect(
   275                      root_scope_id,
   276                      request,
   277                  )
   278              }
   279              other => Err(json!({
   280                  "kind": "direct_run_product_session_event_operation_consumption_pending",
   281                  "reason": "this operation is admitted to the kernel-owned EventJournalProductSession route, but its finite kernel consumption is not built yet; selected work settles as this typed fault instead of a static-executor installation wall",
   282                  "operation": other.as_str(),
   283                  "provider_id": request.provider_id(),
   284                  "backend_session_authority": "EventJournalProductSession",
   285                  "public_host_route": "direct_run.provider_execution_authority",
   286              })
   287              .to_string()),
   288          }
   289      }

   291      fn execute_rust_sdk_publish_event_provider_effect(
   292          root_scope_id: &str,
   293          session_id: &str,
   294          node_id: &str,
   295          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
   296          run_occurred_at_clock: &EventAppendOccurredAtClock,
   297      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, String> {
   298          let selected_publish = swarm_event_publish_append_command_from_provider_input(
   299              root_scope_id,
   300              session_id,
   301              node_id,
   302              request.provider_input(),
   303              run_occurred_at_clock,
   304          )?;
   305          // R41054: the receipt is the checked-law intent echo
   306          // (swarm.event.publish.receipt.v1); it must be minted from the same
   307          // admitted input the journal command consumes, before that input moves.
   308          let receipt_output = swarm_event_publish_receipt_output_for_direct_run_event_owner_v1(
   309              request.provider_input(),
   310          )?;
   311          let transaction =
   312              direct_run_event_swarm_event_publish_transaction_for_append(selected_publish);
   313          let publication_receipt = Self::complete_publication_transaction_for_current_runtime(
   314              transaction,
   315              DirectRunEventPublicationContinuationKind::SwarmEventPublishPrimitive,
   316          )
   317          .map_err(|error| Self::publication_error_diagnostic_value(&error).to_string())?;
   318          let _facts = Self::primary_append_receipt_facts(&publication_receipt)
   319              .map_err(|error| Self::publication_error_diagnostic_value(&error).to_string())?;
   320          request
   321              .into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(receipt_output)
   322              .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
   323      }
   324
   325      // R41079 seam-2 (FIX C): `event.capture()` snapshots the journal HEAD —
   326      // the max-available global seq H, NOT H+1. A publish after the capture
   327      // lands at H+1, and a strictly-after read (`seq > H`) then includes it.
   328      //
   329      // #200 SEAL (ADR-2072 :98 affine handle / :161 body-local start index): the
   330      // captured HEAD is recorded as the IMMUTABLE start index inside the sealed
   331      // body-local capture-handle store, keyed by a freshly minted opaque
   332      // capture_id. The returned handle carries ONLY that capture_id — the cursor
   333      // (after_global_seq) and schema are dropped so a caller can neither read nor
   334      // forge the replay window. The paired read resolves the window from the store,
   335      // never from caller input.
   336      fn execute_ss_test_event_capture_provider_effect(
   337          root_scope_id: &str,
   338          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
   339      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, String> {
   340          let head_global_seq =
   341              direct_run_event_product_route_ss_test_event_capture_head_read(root_scope_id)?;
   342          let capture_id =
   343              direct_run_ss_test_event_capture_handle_store_insert_start_index(head_global_seq)?;
   344          let capture = ProviderValue::Object(
   345              BTreeMap::from([("capture_id".to_owned(), ProviderValue::String(capture_id))]).into(),
   346          );
   347          let output = swarm_ss_test_event_ok_result_carrier(capture);
   348          request
   349              .into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(output)
   350              .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
   351      }
   352
   353      // R41079 seam-2: `event.read({ capture })` replays the events strictly after
   354      // the paired capture window and settles the captured-event batch. The read
   355      // routes the grant-free ss-test range-read; the sealed replay batch emits
   356      // its own final observation (root-admitted) which this owner transports into
   357      // the provider ABI return value, wrapped in the std.Result Ok carrier.
   358      fn execute_ss_test_event_read_provider_effect(
   359          root_scope_id: &str,
   360          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
   361      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, String> {
   362          // #200 SEAL: the read consumes ONLY the opaque capture_id off the handle
   363          // and resolves the IMMUTABLE start index from the sealed body-local store.
   364          // A caller-supplied after_global_seq (forged or otherwise) is never read,
   365          // so the replay window cannot be widened. An unknown/foreign capture_id
   366          // settles a typed fault (rider b) rather than a default cursor.
   367          let capture_id =
   368              swarm_ss_test_event_read_capture_id_from_provider_input(request.provider_input())?;
   369          let start_index = match direct_run_ss_test_event_capture_handle_store_lookup_start_index(
   370              &capture_id,
   371          )? {
   372              Some(start_index) => start_index,
   373              None => {
   374                  return Err(swarm_ss_test_event_read_input_fault(
   375                      "capture.capture_id",
   376                      "event.read capture handle was not minted by event.capture() in this test body; it may be forged or from another body",
   377                  ));
   378              }
   379          };
   380          let batch = direct_run_event_product_route_ss_test_event_capture_range_read(
   381              root_scope_id,
   382              start_index,
   383          )?;
   384          let mut observation = batch
   385              .consume_into_direct_run_ss_test_captured_event_batch_observation_v1(root_scope_id)
   386              .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
   387          // The batch observation carries a raw-cursor capture_id derived from the
   388          // start index; overwrite it with the opaque handle capture_id so the
   389          // caller sees the same sealed id it captured (batch.capture_id ==
   390          // handle.capture_id) and never the underlying cursor.
   391          if let Value::Object(fields) = &mut observation {
   392              fields.insert("capture_id".to_owned(), Value::String(capture_id));
   393          }
   394          let output = swarm_ss_test_event_ok_result_carrier(
   395              provider_value_from_ss_test_event_observation_json(&observation)?,
   396          );
   397          request
   398              .into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(output)
   399              .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
   400      }
   401
   402      // R41120: mintObservationGrant records the sealed grant KERNEL-SIDE. durable-execution-core owns
   403      // the mint + the volatile grantRef->grant registry + admit-by-ref; this host was input-admission
   404      // only. Decode the mint request from provider input, delegate to the grant TYPE owner to validate,
   405      // derive the content-addressed grantRef, and RECORD the sealed grant, then transport the owner-final
   406      // projection (OBS, no authority) to the provider ABI. open's admit-by-ref later locates THIS record
   407      // (both run kernel-side under the ADR-2116 current-thread substrate — same thread_local).
   408      fn execute_swarm_event_mint_observation_grant_provider_effect(
   409          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
   410      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, String> {
   411          let mint_request = swarm_event_mint_observation_grant_request_from_provider_input(
   412              request.provider_input(),
   413          )?;
   414          let projection =
   415              mint_durable_execution_event_observation_grant_v1(mint_request).map_err(|error| {
   416                  json!({
   417                      "kind": "direct_run_event_observation_grant_mint_admission_failed",
   418                      "reason": error.to_string(),
   419                  })
   420                  .to_string()
   421              })?;
   422          let output = provider_value_from_event_observation_grant_projection(projection);
   423          request
   424              .into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(output)
   425              .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
   426      }
   427
   428      // R41120: openObservationStream mints the observation-stream handle. Decode the passed grant's opaque
   429      // grantRef, admit-by-ref against durable-execution-core's record (registry presence = non-forgeability;
   430      // a forged/unrecorded grantRef fails typed), route the landed open executor to create the sealed
   431      // subscription, and emit the handle OBS. The streamRef it returns is the token the R41116 read/control
   432      // arms admit downstream (round-trip closed).
   433      fn execute_ss_test_event_open_observation_stream_provider_effect(
   434          root_scope_id: &str,
   435          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
   436      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, String> {
   437          let provider_input = request.provider_input();
   438          let observation_grant =
   439              swarm_event_observation_admit_grant_from_provider_input(provider_input)?;
   440          let consumer_key =
   441              swarm_event_observation_required_string_from_input(provider_input, "consumerKey")?;
   442          let consumer_instance_id = swarm_event_observation_required_string_from_input(
   443              provider_input,
   444              "consumerInstanceId",
   445          )?;
   446          let default_replay_limit =
   447              swarm_event_observation_replay_limit_from_provider_input(provider_input)?;
   448          let after_global_seq =
   449              swarm_event_observation_after_global_seq_optional_from_provider_input(provider_input)?;
   450          let root_source_ref = observation_grant
   451              .root_source_ref_for_direct_run_event_product_owner_v1()
   452              .to_owned();
   453          let subscription_ref = Self::rust_sdk_open_observation_stream(
   454              root_scope_id,
   455              observation_grant,
   456              consumer_key.clone(),
   457              consumer_instance_id.clone(),
   458              default_replay_limit,
   459              after_global_seq,
   460          )?;
   461          let stream_ref = subscription_ref
   462              .subscription_id_for_direct_run_event_product_owner_v1()
   463              .to_owned();
   464          let handle = ProviderValue::Object(
   465              BTreeMap::from([
   466                  (
   467                      "schema".to_owned(),
   468                      ProviderValue::String("swarm.event.observation_stream_handle.v1".to_owned()),
   469                  ),
   470                  ("streamRef".to_owned(), ProviderValue::String(stream_ref)),
   471                  (
   472                      "rootSourceRef".to_owned(),
   473                      ProviderValue::String(root_source_ref),
   474                  ),
   475                  (
   476                      "consumerKey".to_owned(),
   477                      ProviderValue::String(consumer_key),
   478                  ),
   479                  (
   480                      "consumerInstanceId".to_owned(),
   481                      ProviderValue::String(consumer_instance_id),
   482                  ),
   483              ])
   484              .into(),
   485          );
   486          let output = swarm_ss_test_event_ok_result_carrier(handle);
   487          request
   488              .into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(output)
   489              .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
   490      }
   491
   492      // R41116 (a-subset): the streamRef-driven observation-stream reads. Each
   493      // decodes the opaque streamRef handle from provider_input, LOCATES the sealed
   494      // subscription via admit_..._from_provider_stream_ref (registry presence =
   495      // non-forgeability; unrecorded streamRef fails typed), routes the landed
   496      // executor, and transports the owner-minted final OBS to the provider ABI.
   497      // Open + control/add_observation_grant stay consumption_pending (grant-gated,
   498      // R41120). In production all 20 files die at Open first, so these are proven
   499      // by cfg(test) unit tests via the test-grant builder; corpus proof rides
   500      // R41120's landing.
   501      fn execute_ss_test_event_read_observation_stream_provider_effect(
   502          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
   503      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, String> {
   504          let stream_ref = swarm_ss_test_event_observation_stream_ref_from_provider_input(
   505              request.provider_input(),
   506          )?;
   507          let max_frames = swarm_ss_test_event_observation_max_frames_from_provider_input(
   508              request.provider_input(),
   509          )?;
   510          // waitMs is a contracted optional delivery HINT (ADR-2128 note 160): it must cross the
   511          // finite unsigned-integer admission aperture and must not be parsed-and-ignored. This
   512          // synchronous read has no wait authority to consult, so the validated value is discarded.
   513          let _wait_ms =
   514              swarm_ss_test_event_observation_wait_ms_from_provider_input(request.provider_input())?;
   515          let subscription_ref =
   516              admit_direct_run_event_product_subscription_ref_from_provider_stream_ref_for_direct_run_event_product_owner_v1(
   517                  &stream_ref,
   518                  DirectRunEventSubscriptionRegistryEntryReadAuthority::for_product_api_admission(
   519                      DirectRunEventProductApiAdmissionOperation::RustSdkReadObservationStream,
   520                  ),
   521              )?;
   522          let (batch, acked_global_seq, consumer_instance_id) =
   523              direct_run_event_product_route_rust_sdk_read_observation_stream(
   524                  subscription_ref,
   525                  max_frames,
   526              )?;
   527          let observation = direct_run_event_observation_stream_read_result_from_replay_batch(
   528              &stream_ref,
   529              &consumer_instance_id,
   530              acked_global_seq,
   531              &batch,
   532          )?;
   533          let output = swarm_ss_test_event_ok_result_carrier(
   534              provider_value_from_ss_test_event_observation_json(&observation)?,
   535          );
   536          request
   537              .into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(output)
   538              .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
   539      }
   540
   541      fn execute_ss_test_event_read_observation_source_registry_provider_effect(
   542          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,

   544          let stream_ref = swarm_ss_test_event_observation_stream_ref_from_provider_input(

   565      fn execute_ss_test_event_read_observation_cursor_provider_effect(
   566          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
   567      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, String> {
   568          let stream_ref = swarm_ss_test_event_observation_stream_ref_from_provider_input(
   569              request.provider_input(),
   570          )?;
   571          let subscription_ref =
   572              admit_direct_run_event_product_subscription_ref_from_provider_stream_ref_for_direct_run_event_product_owner_v1(
   573                  &stream_ref,
   574                  DirectRunEventSubscriptionRegistryEntryReadAuthority::for_product_api_admission(
   575                      DirectRunEventProductApiAdmissionOperation::RustSdkReadObservationCursor,
   576                  ),
   577              )?;
   578          let cursor_global_seq =
   579              direct_run_event_product_route_rust_sdk_read_observation_cursor(subscription_ref)?;
   580          let observation =
   581              direct_run_event_observation_cursor_projection_v1(&stream_ref, Some(cursor_global_seq));
   582          let output = swarm_ss_test_event_ok_result_carrier(
   583              provider_value_from_ss_test_event_observation_json(&observation)?,
   584          );

   589
   590      fn execute_ss_test_event_control_observation_stream_provider_effect(
   591          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
   592      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner, String> {
   593          let stream_ref = swarm_ss_test_event_observation_stream_ref_from_provider_input(
   594              request.provider_input(),
   595          )?;
   596          let operation = swarm_ss_test_event_observation_operation_from_provider_input(
   597              request.provider_input(),
   598          )?;
   599          // R41120: add_observation_grant locates the subscription by streamRef (R41116 admit-by-streamRef),
   600          // admits the passed grant by ref (R41120 producer; forged/unrecorded fails typed), applies the
   601          // landed add-grant route (records the source into the subscription), and emits the control receipt.
   602          if operation == "add_observation_grant" {
   603              let subscription_ref =
   604                  admit_direct_run_event_product_subscription_ref_from_provider_stream_ref_for_direct_run_event_product_owner_v1(
   605                      &stream_ref,
   606                      DirectRunEventSubscriptionRegistryEntryReadAuthority::for_product_api_admission(
   607                          DirectRunEventProductApiAdmissionOperation::RustSdkControlObservationStream,
   608                      ),
   609                  )?;
   610              let observation_grant =
   611                  swarm_event_observation_admit_grant_from_provider_input(request.provider_input())?;
   612              Self::rust_sdk_control_observation_stream_add_grant(
   613                  subscription_ref,
   614                  observation_grant,
   615              )?;
   616              let receipt = ProviderValue::Object(
   617                  BTreeMap::from([
   618                      (
   619                          "schema".to_owned(),
   620                          ProviderValue::String(
   621                              "swarm.event.observation_stream_control_receipt.v1".to_owned(),
   622                          ),
   623                      ),
   624                      ("streamRef".to_owned(), ProviderValue::String(stream_ref)),
   625                      (
   626                          "operation".to_owned(),
   627                          ProviderValue::String("add_observation_grant".to_owned()),
   628                      ),
   629                      ("accepted".to_owned(), ProviderValue::Bool(true)),
   630                  ])
   631                  .into(),
   632              );
   633              let output = swarm_ss_test_event_ok_result_carrier(receipt);
   634              return request
   635                  .into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(output)
   636                  .map_err(|error| error.to_string());
   637          }
   638          let subscription_ref =
   639              admit_direct_run_event_product_subscription_ref_from_provider_stream_ref_for_direct_run_event_product_owner_v1(
   640                  &stream_ref,
   641                  DirectRunEventSubscriptionRegistryEntryReadAuthority::for_product_api_admission(
   642                      DirectRunEventProductApiAdmissionOperation::RustSdkControlObservationStream,
   643                  ),
   644              )?;
   645          let projection = match operation.as_str() {
   646              "close_stream" => {
   647                  direct_run_event_product_route_rust_sdk_control_observation_stream_close(
   648                      subscription_ref,
   649                  )?
   650              }
   651              "mask_source" | "unmask_source" | "remove_source" => {
   652                  let source_ref = swarm_ss_test_event_observation_source_ref_from_provider_input(
   653                      request.provider_input(),
   654                  )?;
   655                  let source_operation: &'static str = match operation.as_str() {
   656                      "mask_source" => "mask_source",
   657                      "unmask_source" => "unmask_source",
   658                      _ => "remove_source",
   659                  };
   660                  direct_run_event_product_route_rust_sdk_control_observation_stream_source_control(
   661                      subscription_ref,
   662                      source_ref,
   663                      source_operation,
   664                  )?
   665              }
   666              _ => {
   667                  return Err(swarm_ss_test_event_read_input_fault(
   668                      "operation",
   669                      "control_observation_stream operation must be close_stream, mask_source, unmask_source, remove_source, or add_observation_grant",
   670                  ));
   671              }
   672          };
   673          let output = swarm_ss_test_event_ok_result_carrier(
   674              provider_value_from_ss_test_event_observation_json(&projection)?,
   675          );
   676          request
   677              .into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(output)
   678              .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
   679      }

   216      fn diagnostic_kind_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(&self) -> &'static str {
   217          match self {
   218              Self::SelectedBoundaryTake => "process_child_selected_boundary_take_fault",
   219              Self::RouteSelection => "process_child_provider_route_selection_fault",
   220              Self::ProcessLoadExecute => "process_child_process_load_execute_fault",

  1387  fn commit_process_child_provider_drive_result_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
  1388      session: &mut EngineLiveProcessSessionV1,
  1389      provider_drive_result: ProviderDriveResult,
  1390  ) -> Result<EngineProcessSessionRunResultV1, crate::ProviderDriveSessionExecutionCommitFault> {
  1391      let (outcome, output_effect_drain_receipts) = provider_drive_result
  1392          .commit_ready_into_session_execution_kernel_and_drive_to_direct_run_result_product_v1(
  1393              session,
  1394              "direct_process_child_provider_resume_ready_output",
  1395          )?;
  1396      let (observations, process_output_records) =
  1397          crate::direct_run::event::DirectRunEventPublicationBackendOutputDrainObservationBundle::from_provider_drive_output_effect_drain_receipts_and_process_output_records_for_direct_run_event_publication_owner_v1(
  1398              output_effect_drain_receipts,
  1399          );
  1400      Ok(
  1401          admit_process_child_engine_result_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
  1402              session,
  1403              outcome,
  1404              "direct_process_child_provider_resume_ready_output",
  1405          )
  1406          .with_event_publication_backend_output_drain_observations_for_direct_run_owner_v1(
  1407              observations,
  1408          )
  1409          .with_provider_process_output_records_for_direct_run_owner_v1(process_output_records),
  1410      )

  1586  fn drive_process_child_selected_provider_boundary_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
  1587      mut frame: DirectRunProcessChildSessionFrameV1,
  1588      selected_boundary: crate::SelectedProviderResumeBoundaryForDirectRunOwnerV1,
  1589      provider_execution_session: &mut ProviderHostExecutionSession,
  1590  ) -> DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1 {
  1591      macro_rules! string_fault {
  1592          ($result:expr, $phase:expr) => {
  1593              match $result {
  1594                  Ok(value) => value,
  1595                  Err(failure) => {
  1596                      return DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1::Fault(
  1597                          DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultV1::Frame {
  1598                              frame,
  1599                              phase: $phase,
  1600                              failure,
  1601                          },
  1602                      );
  1603                  }
  1604              }
  1605          };
  1606      }
  1607
  1608      let selected_provider_input = string_fault!(
  1609          frame
  1610              .session
  1611              .take_selected_provider_resume_host_input_for_direct_run_owner_v1(selected_boundary),
  1612          DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultPhaseV1::SelectedBoundaryTake
  1613      );
  1614      let selected_route = string_fault!(
  1615          select_provider_resume_route_for_direct_run_owner_v1(selected_provider_input),
  1616          DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultPhaseV1::RouteSelection
  1617      );
  1618      match selected_route {
  1619          DirectRunSelectedProviderResumeRouteV1::ProcessLoad(selected_input) => {
  1620              let admitted_load = string_fault!(
  1621                  frame
  1622                      .session
  1623                      .admit_selected_process_load_child_launch_for_direct_run_owner_v1(
  1624                          selected_input,
  1625                      )
  1626                      .map_err(|fault| fault.to_string()),
  1627                  DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultPhaseV1::ProcessLoadExecute
  1628              );
  1629              let provider_drive_result =
  1630                  match execute_kernel_internal_process_load_with_static_child_context_for_provider_resume_owner_v1(
  1631                      admitted_load
  1632                          .commit_into_process_lifecycle_registration_for_direct_run_owner_v1(),
  1633                      frame.drive_context.root_scope_id(),
  1634                      frame.drive_context.live_process_session_id(),
  1635                      frame.drive_context.current_process(),
  1636                  ) {
  1637                      Ok(provider_drive_result) => provider_drive_result,
  1638                      Err(refusal) => {
  1639                          return DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1::Fault(
  1640                              DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultV1::ProcessLoad { frame, refusal },
  1641                          );
  1642                      }
  1643                  };
  1644              let result =
  1645                  match commit_process_child_provider_drive_result_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
  1646                      &mut frame.session,
  1647                      provider_drive_result,
  1648                  ) {
  1649                      Ok(result) => result,
  1650                      Err(fault) => {
  1651                          return DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1::Fault(
  1652                              DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultV1::ProviderDriveCommit {
  1653                                  frame,
  1654                                  fault,
  1655                              },
  1656                          );
  1657                      }
  1658                  };
  1659              continue_process_child_provider_transition_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(frame, result)
  1660          }
  1661          DirectRunSelectedProviderResumeRouteV1::ProcessCheckpoint(selected_input) => {
  1662              let provider_drive_result =
  1663                  match execute_kernel_internal_process_checkpoint_for_provider_resume_owner_v1(
  1664                      selected_input,
  1665                  ) {
  1666                      Ok(provider_drive_result) => provider_drive_result,
  1667                      Err(refusal) => {
  1668                          return DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1::Fault(
  1669                              DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultV1::ProcessCheckpoint {
  1670                                  frame,
  1671                                  refusal,
  1672                              },
  1673                          );
  1674                      }
  1675                  };
  1676              let result =
  1677                  match commit_process_child_provider_drive_result_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
  1678                      &mut frame.session,
  1679                      provider_drive_result,
  1680                  ) {
  1681                      Ok(result) => result,
  1682                      Err(fault) => {
  1683                          return DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1::Fault(
  1684                              DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultV1::ProviderDriveCommit {
  1685                                  frame,
  1686                                  fault,

  1688                          );
  1689                      }

  1692          }
  1693          DirectRunSelectedProviderResumeRouteV1::ProcessRestore(selected_input) => {

  1701                              DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultV1::ProcessRestore {
  1702                                  frame,
  1703                                  refusal,
  1704                              },
  1705                          );
  1706                      }
  1707                  };
  1708              let result =

  1710                      &mut frame.session,

  1713                      Ok(result) => result,

  1715                          return DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1::Fault(

  1719                              },
  1720                          );
  1721                      }

  1723              continue_process_child_provider_transition_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(frame, result)

  1725          DirectRunSelectedProviderResumeRouteV1::ProcessInvoke(selected_input) => {

  1729                      .duplicate_for_nested_process_child_owner_v1(),

  1784                      .duplicate_for_nested_process_child_owner_v1(),

  1790              {

  1804                  DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultPhaseV1::ProcessRunPrepare
  1805              );
  1806              let child = match start_prepared_process_run_child_session_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(

  1822              complete_prepared_process_run_descent_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(

  1848              let child =

  1892                      frame.drive_context.live_process_session_id(),
  1893                      frame.drive_context.node_id(),
  1894                      admitted_request,
  1895                      &durable_execution_core::EventAppendOccurredAtClock::from_run_started_at(
  1896                          frame.drive_context.started_at(),
  1897                      ),
  1898                      ),
  1899                      DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultPhaseV1::EventHostExecute
  1900                  )
  1901              } else {
  1902                  match provider_execution_session
  1903                      .invoke_selected_provider_boundary_request_for_direct_run_owner_v1(
  1904                          admitted_request,
  1905                      ) {
  1906                      Ok(execution_result) => execution_result,
  1907                      Err(fault) => {
  1908                          return DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1::Fault(
  1909                              DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultV1::HostExecution { frame, fault },
  1910                          );
  1911                      }
  1912                  }
  1913              };
  1914              let provider_drive_result = match ProviderDriveResult::ready_from_rust_sdk_static_provider_execution_result_for_provider_drive_result_owner_v1(execution_result) {
  1915                  Ok(provider_drive_result) => provider_drive_result,
  1916                  Err(fault) => {
  1917                      return DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1::Fault(
  1918                          DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultV1::HostResultAdmission { frame, fault },
  1919                      );
  1920                  }
  1921              };
  1922              let result =
  1923                  match commit_process_child_provider_drive_result_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
  1924                      &mut frame.session,
  1925                      provider_drive_result,
  1926                  ) {
  1927                      Ok(result) => result,
  1928                      Err(fault) => {
  1929                          return DirectRunProcessChildProviderTransitionV1::Fault(
  1930                              DirectRunProcessChildProviderFaultV1::ProviderDriveCommit {
  1931                                  frame,
  1932                                  fault,
  1933                              },
  1934                          );
  1935                      }
  1936                  };
  1937              continue_process_child_provider_transition_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(frame, result)
  1938          }
  1939      }
  1940  }

## crates/ss-runtime-source-compiler-owner/src/direct_run/runtime_authority/process_session_public_aperture/provider_resume_lifecycle.rs:1-35

- Full-file bytes: 74876

     1  use super::*;
     2
     3  pub(super) fn exact_process_builtin_contract_identity_for_kernel_internal_route(
     4      identity_cell: &'static OnceLock<
     5          Result<swarm_capability_model::CapabilityContractIdentity, String>,
     6      >,
     7      export_name: &'static str,
     8  ) -> Result<
     9      (
    10          &'static swarm_capability_model::CapabilityContractIdentity,
    11          &'static str,
    12      ),
    13      String,
    14  > {
    15      let identity = identity_cell.get_or_init(|| {
    16          let binding = RustSdkBuiltinProviderCatalogue::builtin_for_static_provider_host_owner_v1()
    17              .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?
    18              .into_provider_bindings()
    19              .into_iter()
    20              .find(|binding| {
    21                  binding.package_specifier() == SWARM_PROCESS_MODULE_ID
    22                      && binding.export_name() == export_name
    23              })
    24              .ok_or_else(|| {
    25                  format!(
    26                      "builtin provider catalogue has no exact @swarm/process:{export_name} binding"
    27                  )
    28              })?;
    29          binding
    30              .sealed_identity_for_static_provider_host_owner_v1()
    31              .ok_or_else(|| {
    32                  format!(
    33                      "builtin @swarm/process:{export_name} binding did not retain its sealed contract identity"
    34                  )
    35              })

     1  use super::provider_resume_lifecycle::*;
     2  use super::session_route_lifecycle::*;
     3  use super::*;
     4
     5  fn direct_run_public_aperture_next_step_without_closed_drive_consumer_fault(
     6      owner_kind: DirectRunKernelStateRefOwnerKind,
     7  ) -> String {
     8      json!({
     9          "kind": "direct_run_public_aperture_next_step_owner_kind_without_closed_drive_consumer",
    10          "reason": "selected body launch closed drive requires a finite owner consumer for each public-aperture next step",
    11          "owner_kind": owner_kind.as_str(),
    12      })
    13      .to_string()
    14  }
    15
    16  impl DirectRunRuntimeAuthorityOwner {
    17      pub(in crate::direct_run) fn drive_prepared_runtime_process_start_command_public_aperture_until_terminal_or_next_step_with_runtime_terminal_observation_for_ss_test_owner_v1(
    18          command: DirectSwarmScriptRunPublicAperturePreparedRuntimeProcessStartCommand,
    19      ) -> Result<
    20          DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureDriveOutputV1,
    21          DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1,
    22      > {
    23          let (kernel_state, prepared_runtime_executable_image) = command
    24              .into_start_product_for_direct_run_public_aperture_owner_v1()?
    25              .into_kernel_state_and_session_open_inputs_for_direct_run_runtime_authority_owner_v1(
    26              )?;
    27          Self::drive_process_start_kernel_state_public_aperture_until_terminal_or_next_step(
    28              kernel_state,
    29              prepared_runtime_executable_image,
    30              "prepared_runtime_process_start_public_aperture",
    31          )
    32      }
    33
    34      pub(in crate::direct_run) fn drive_prepared_runtime_process_start_command_public_aperture_until_terminal_with_runtime_terminal_observation_for_ss_test_owner_v1(
    35          command: DirectSwarmScriptRunPublicAperturePreparedRuntimeProcessStartCommand,
    36          provider_execution_session: &mut ProviderHostExecutionSession,
    37      ) -> Result<
    38          DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureOutputEmissionProductV1,
    39          DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1,
    40      > {
    41          let mut output =
    42              Self::drive_prepared_runtime_process_start_command_public_aperture_until_terminal_or_next_step_with_runtime_terminal_observation_for_ss_test_owner_v1(
    43                  command,
    44              )?;
    45          loop {
    46              match output.into_closed_drive_state_for_direct_run_runtime_execution_owner_v1() {
    47                  DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureClosedDriveStateV1::Terminal(output) => {
    48                      return Ok(output);
    49                  }
    50                  DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureClosedDriveStateV1::NextStep(next_step) => {
    51                      output = Self::drive_public_aperture_next_step_until_terminal_or_next_step(
    52                          next_step,
    53                          provider_execution_session,
    54                      )?;
    55                  }
    56                  DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureClosedDriveStateV1::HostResourceFinalization(
    57                      finalization,
    58                  ) => {
    59                      output = Self::drive_host_resource_finalization_until_terminal_or_next_step(
    60                          finalization,
    61                          provider_execution_session,
    62                      )
    63                      .map_err(DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1::Generic)?;
    64                  }
    65                  DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureClosedDriveStateV1::ProcessInvokeAwaitExecution(
    66                      boundary,
    67                  ) => {
    68                      output = Self::drive_process_invoke_await_execution_until_terminal_or_next_step(
    69                          boundary,
    70                          provider_execution_session,
    71                      )?;
    72                  }
    73                  DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureClosedDriveStateV1::ProcessRunDriveTerminal(
    74                      boundary,
    75                  ) => {
    76                      output = Self::drive_process_run_drive_terminal_until_terminal_or_next_step(
    77                          boundary,
    78                          provider_execution_session,
    79                      )?;
    80                  }
    81                  DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureClosedDriveStateV1::ProcessControl(
    82                      boundary,
    83                  ) => {
    84                      output = finish_direct_run_public_aperture_drive_output(
    85                          boundary
    86                              .drive_for_process_kernel_owner_v1()
    87                              .map_err(DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1::Generic)?,
    88                      )
    89                      .map_err(DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1::Generic)?;
    90                  }
    91              }
    92          }
    93      }
    94
    95      fn drive_public_aperture_next_step_until_terminal_or_next_step(
    96          next_step: DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureNextStepOutputProductV1,
    97          provider_execution_session: &mut ProviderHostExecutionSession,
    98      ) -> Result<
    99          DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureDriveOutputV1,
   100          DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1,
   101      > {
   102          let owner_kind = next_step
   103              .owner_kind_for_direct_run_process_session_public_aperture_owner_v1(
   104                  "direct_run_public_aperture_closed_drive.next_step.owner_kind",
   105              )?;
   106          match owner_kind {
   107              DirectRunKernelStateRefOwnerKind::ProviderResume => {
   108                  Self::drive_provider_resume_public_aperture_next_step_until_terminal_or_next_step(
   109                      next_step,
   110                      provider_execution_session,
   111                  )
   112              }
   113              DirectRunKernelStateRefOwnerKind::ProcessSessionStart
   114              | DirectRunKernelStateRefOwnerKind::ProcessSessionReawaken
   115              | DirectRunKernelStateRefOwnerKind::ProcessSessionProjection => {
   116                  let kernel_state_ref = next_step
   117                      .into_kernel_state_ref_for_direct_run_process_session_public_aperture_owner_v1(
   118                      );
   119                  drive_process_session_continuation_ref_to_public_aperture_with_typed_live_authority(
   120                      kernel_state_ref,
   121                  )
   122              }
   123              other => Err(DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1::Generic(
   124                  direct_run_public_aperture_next_step_without_closed_drive_consumer_fault(other),
   125              )),
   126          }
   127      }
   128
   129      fn drive_host_resource_finalization_until_terminal_or_next_step(
   130          finalization: DirectRunHostResourceFinalizationNextStepV1,
   131          provider_execution_session: &mut ProviderHostExecutionSession,
   132      ) -> Result<DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureDriveOutputV1, String> {
   133          let route_output =
   134              finalization.drive_for_direct_run_owner_v1(provider_execution_session)?;
   135          Ok(finish_direct_run_public_aperture_drive_output(
   136              route_output,
   137          )?)
   138      }
   139
   140      fn drive_process_invoke_await_execution_until_terminal_or_next_step(
   141          next_step: DirectRunProcessInvokeAwaitExecutionNextStepV1,
   142          provider_execution_session: &mut ProviderHostExecutionSession,
   143      ) -> Result<
   144          DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureDriveOutputV1,
   145          DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1,
   146      > {
   147          let route_output = next_step
   148              .into_child_drive_stage_for_process_kernel_owner_v1()
   149              .drive_matching_child_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(provider_execution_session)?
   150              .commit_and_route_parent_for_process_kernel_owner_v1()?;
   151          Ok(finish_direct_run_public_aperture_drive_output(
   152              route_output,
   153          )?)
   154      }
   155
   156      fn drive_process_run_drive_terminal_until_terminal_or_next_step(
   157          next_step: DirectRunProcessRunDriveTerminalNextStepV1,
   158          provider_execution_session: &mut ProviderHostExecutionSession,
   159      ) -> Result<
   160          DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureDriveOutputV1,
   161          DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1,
   162      > {
   163          let route_output = next_step
   164              .into_child_drive_stage_for_process_kernel_owner_v1()
   165              .drive_matching_child_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(provider_execution_session)?
   166              .commit_and_route_parent_for_process_kernel_owner_v1()?;
   167          Ok(finish_direct_run_public_aperture_drive_output(
   168              route_output,
   169          )?)
   170      }
   171
   172      fn drive_provider_resume_public_aperture_next_step_until_terminal_or_next_step(
   173          next_step: DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureNextStepOutputProductV1,
   174          provider_execution_session: &mut ProviderHostExecutionSession,
   175      ) -> Result<
   176          DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureDriveOutputV1,
   177          DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1,
   178      > {
   179          let kernel_state_ref = next_step
   180              .into_kernel_state_ref_for_direct_run_process_session_public_aperture_owner_v1();
   181          let token_admission_operation =
   182              "direct_run_public_aperture_closed_drive.provider_resume.typed_token_admission";
   183          let mut provider_resume_token = kernel_state_ref
   184              .provider_resume_continuation_token_for_runtime_authority_owner(
   185                  token_admission_operation,
   186              )?;
   187          let provider_resume_private_storage =
   188              take_provider_resume_host_boundary_private_storage_from_public_aperture_kernel_state_ref_for_runtime_authority_owner(
   189                  kernel_state_ref,
   190                  &provider_resume_token,
   191                  token_admission_operation,
   192                  "direct_run_public_aperture_closed_drive.provider_resume.private_storage_take",
   193              )?;
   194          let selected_boundary = provider_resume_token
   195              .take_provider_resume_boundary_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1()?;
   196          let selected_provider_input =
   197              Self::take_selected_provider_resume_host_input_for_live_process_session(
   198                  provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),

   202          let selected_route =
   203              select_provider_resume_route_for_direct_run_owner_v1(selected_provider_input)?;

   231                      .map_err(DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1::ProcessCheckpoint)?;
   232                  Self::apply_provider_drive_ready_result_for_live_process_session(
