   235                      provider_drive_result,
   236                  )?
   237              }
   238              DirectRunSelectedProviderResumeRouteV1::ProcessRestore(selected_input) => {
   239                  let provider_drive_result =
   240                      execute_kernel_internal_process_restore_with_static_child_context_for_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   241                          selected_input,
   242                      )
   243                      .map_err(DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1::ProcessRestore)?;
   244                  Self::apply_provider_drive_ready_result_for_live_process_session(
   245                      provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   246                      provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   247                      provider_drive_result,
   248                  )?
   249              }
   250              DirectRunSelectedProviderResumeRouteV1::ProcessInvoke(selected_input) => {
   251                  let drive_context =
   252                      DirectRunProcessKernelChildDriveContext::from_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   253                          provider_resume_private_storage.current_process(),
   254                          provider_resume_private_storage.admitted_static_child_source_programs(),
   255                          provider_resume_private_storage.prepared_static_child_runtime_handles(),
   256                          provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   257                          provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   258                          provider_resume_token.node_id(),
   259                          provider_resume_token.started_at(),
   260                      )?;
   261                  let admitted_launch =
   262                      Self::admit_selected_process_invoke_child_launch_for_live_process_session(
   263                          provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   264                          provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   265                          selected_input,
   266                      )?;
   267                  let PreparedProcessInvokeProviderIngressV1 {
   268                      ingress,
   269                      registration,
   270                      execution,
   271                  } = prepare_process_invoke_provider_ingress_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
   272                      admitted_launch,
   273                  )?;
   274                  let engine_result =
   275                      Self::commit_process_invoke_execution_provider_ingress_for_live_process_session(
   276                          provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   277                          provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   278                          ingress,
   279                      )?;
   280                  if engine_result.outcome_kind() != "process_invoke_await_execution" {
   281                      let _settled_unselected_registration = (registration, execution, drive_context);
   282                      return Err(json!({
   283                          "kind": "process_invoke_nominal_ingress_outcome_mismatch",
   284                          "reason": "committed process.invoke nominal ingress must yield its exact await-execution boundary before execution registration",
   285                          "outcome_kind": engine_result.outcome_kind(),
   286                      })
   287                      .to_string()
   288                      .into());
   289                  }
   290                  register_process_invoke_execution_after_ingress_commit_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
   291                      registration,
   292                      execution,
   293                      drive_context,
   294                  )?;
   295                  engine_result
   296              }
   297              DirectRunSelectedProviderResumeRouteV1::ProcessRun(selected_input) => {
   298                  let drive_context =
   299                      DirectRunProcessKernelChildDriveContext::from_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   300                          provider_resume_private_storage.current_process(),
   301                          provider_resume_private_storage.admitted_static_child_source_programs(),
   302                          provider_resume_private_storage.prepared_static_child_runtime_handles(),
   303                          provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   304                          provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   305                          provider_resume_token.node_id(),
   306                          provider_resume_token.started_at(),
   307                      )?;
   308                  let admitted_launch =
   309                      Self::admit_selected_process_run_child_launch_for_live_process_session(
   310                          provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   311                          provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   312                          selected_input,
   313                      )?;
   314                  let PreparedProcessRunProviderIngressV1 {
   315                      ingress,
   316                      registration,
   317                      execution,
   318                  } = prepare_process_run_provider_ingress_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
   319                      admitted_launch,
   320                  )?;
   321                  let engine_result =
   322                      Self::commit_process_run_child_provider_ingress_for_live_process_session(
   323                          provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   324                          provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   325                          ingress,
   326                      )?;
   327                  if engine_result.outcome_kind() != "process_run_drive_terminal" {
   328                      let _settled_unselected_registration = (registration, execution, drive_context);
   329                      return Err(json!({
   330                          "kind": "process_run_nominal_ingress_outcome_mismatch",
   331                          "reason": "committed process.run nominal ingress must yield its exact drive-terminal boundary before child registration",
   332                          "outcome_kind": engine_result.outcome_kind(),
   333                      })
   334                      .to_string()
   335                      .into());
   336                  }
   337                  register_process_run_child_after_ingress_commit_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
   338                      registration,
   339                      execution,
   340                      drive_context,
   341                  )?;
   342                  engine_result
   343              }
   344              DirectRunSelectedProviderResumeRouteV1::ProcessActivate(selected_input) => {
   345                  let drive_context =
   346                      DirectRunProcessKernelChildDriveContext::from_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   347                          provider_resume_private_storage.current_process(),
   348                          provider_resume_private_storage.admitted_static_child_source_programs(),
   349                          provider_resume_private_storage.prepared_static_child_runtime_handles(),
   350                          provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   351                          provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   352                          provider_resume_token.node_id(),
   353                          provider_resume_token.started_at(),
   354                      )?;
   355                  let PreparedProcessActivateProviderIngressV1 {
   356                      ingress,
   357                      registration,
   358                      execution,
   359                      lifecycle_recovery,
   360                  } = prepare_process_activate_provider_ingress_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
   361                      selected_input,
   362                  )?;
   363                  let engine_result =
   364                      Self::commit_process_run_child_provider_ingress_for_live_process_session(
   365                          provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   366                          provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   367                          ingress,
   368                      )?;
   369                  register_process_activate_child(
   370                      registration,
   371                      execution,
   372                      lifecycle_recovery,
   373                      drive_context,
   374                  )
   375                  .map_err(|refusal| {
   376                      let recovery = refusal.lifecycle_recovery;
   377                      match refusal.execution {
   378                          DirectRunProcessActivateChildExecutionV1::Prepared(execution) => {
   379                              let cancelled = execution
   380                                  .cancel_before_process_activate_registry_commit_for_process_lifecycle_owner_v1();
   381                              let (open_plan, loaded_process) = cancelled
   382                                  .into_ready_lifecycle_custody_for_process_lifecycle_owner_v1();
   383                              let restoration = register_process_lifecycle_after_control_for_process_kernel_owner_v1(
   384                                  recovery.authority,
   385                                  recovery.activation_process_carrier,
   386                                  loaded_process,
   387                                  open_plan,
   388                                  recovery.checkpoint_state,
   389                              );
   390                              match restoration {
   391                                  Ok(()) => "process.activate execution registry refused complete custody; lifecycle restored".to_owned(),
   392                                  Err(failure) => failure,
   393                              }
   394                          }
   395                          DirectRunProcessActivateChildExecutionV1::RetryOpen(open_refusal) => {
   396                              match register_process_activate_open_refusal(
   397                                  recovery.authority,
   398                                  recovery.activation_process_carrier,
   399                                  open_refusal,
   400                                  recovery.checkpoint_state,
   401                              ) {
   402                                  Ok(()) => "process.activate exact open remains in retryable lifecycle custody".to_owned(),
   403                                  Err(retained) => {
   404                                      let recovery = super::super::process_kernel_boundary::DirectRunProcessActivateLifecycleRecoveryV1 {
   405                                          authority: retained.authority,
   406                                          activation_process_carrier: retained.activation_process_carrier,
   407                                          checkpoint_state: retained.checkpoint_state,
   408                                      };
   409                                      let cancellation = retained
   410                                          .refusal
   411                                          .cancel_for_process_lifecycle_owner_v1();
   412                                      recovery
   413                                          .settle_with_transitioned_open_cancellation_for_process_lifecycle_owner_v1(cancellation)
   414                                          .to_string()
   415                                  }
   416                              }
   417                          }
   418                      }
   419                  })?;
   420                  engine_result
   421              }
   422              DirectRunSelectedProviderResumeRouteV1::ProviderHost(selected_host_input) => {
   423                  let admitted_request = selected_host_input
   424                      .admit_host_typed_request_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   425                          provider_execution_session,
   426                      )
   427                      .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
   428                  let execution_result = if swarm_event_provider_requires_product_session_boundary(
   429                      admitted_request.provider_id(),
   430                  ) {
   431                      crate::direct_run::event::DirectRunEventProductOwner::execute_selected_product_session_provider_effect_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   432                          provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   433                          provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   434                          provider_resume_token.node_id(),
   435                          admitted_request,
   436                          &durable_execution_core::EventAppendOccurredAtClock::from_run_started_at(
   437                              provider_resume_token.started_at(),
   438                          ),
   439                      )?
   440                  } else {
   441                      provider_execution_session
   442                          .invoke_selected_provider_boundary_request_for_direct_run_owner_v1(
   443                              admitted_request,
   444                          )
   445                          .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?
   446                  };
   447                  let provider_drive_result = ProviderDriveResult::ready_from_rust_sdk_static_provider_execution_result_for_provider_drive_result_owner_v1(
   448                      execution_result,
   449                  )
   450                  .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
   451                  Self::apply_provider_drive_ready_result_for_live_process_session(
   452                      provider_resume_token.live_process_session_id(),
   453                      provider_resume_token.root_scope_id(),
   454                      provider_drive_result,
   455                  )?
   456              }
   457          };
   458          let route_output =
   459              route_engine_process_session_result_for_public_aperture_provider_resume_owner(
   460                  provider_resume_token,
   461                  provider_resume_private_storage,
   462                  engine_result,
   463              )?;
   464          finish_direct_run_public_aperture_drive_output(route_output)
   465              .map_err(DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1::Generic)
   466      }
   467
   468      fn drive_process_start_kernel_state_public_aperture_until_terminal_or_next_step(
   469          mut kernel_state: DirectSwarmScriptRunKernelState,
   470          prepared_runtime_executable_image: EngineInstalledPreparedSessionRuntimeV1,
   471          aperture_label: &'static str,
   472      ) -> Result<
   473          DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureDriveOutputV1,
   474          DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1,
   475      > {
   476          let root_input = direct_run_take_process_session_root_input(&mut kernel_state)?;
   477          Self::drive_process_start_kernel_state_public_aperture_with_root_input_until_terminal_or_next_step(
   478              kernel_state,
   479              prepared_runtime_executable_image,
   480              root_input,
   481              aperture_label,
   482          )
   483      }
   484
   485      fn drive_process_start_kernel_state_public_aperture_with_root_input_until_terminal_or_next_step(
   486          kernel_state: DirectSwarmScriptRunKernelState,
   487          prepared_runtime_executable_image: EngineInstalledPreparedSessionRuntimeV1,
   488          root_input: EngineVmObjectValueV1,
   489          aperture_label: &'static str,
   490      ) -> Result<
   491          DirectRunProcessSessionPublicApertureDriveOutputV1,
   492          DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1,
   493      > {
   494          let durability_policy =
   495              direct_run_process_session_durability_policy_for_public_aperture_owner(
   496                  kernel_state.launch_durability_policy,
   497              )?;
   498          let process_identity = kernel_state
   499              .current_process
   500              .as_ref()
   501              .ok_or_else(|| {
   502                  "prepared-runtime public-aperture process session open requires the launch owner's admitted current-process identity; the fresh direct-run launch path must install it before open"
   503                      .to_owned()
   504              })?
   505              .process_session_identity_for_session_runtime_open_owner_v1()?;
   506          let live_session =
   507              open_process_session_v0_from_exact_static_child_dispatch_installed_prepared_runtime_for_direct_run_owner_v1(
   508                  prepared_runtime_executable_image,
   509                  crate::ProcessSessionInitialInputForDirectRunOwnerV1::from_root_input_for_direct_run_owner_v1(root_input),
   510                  durability_policy,
   511                  process_identity,
   512              )
   513              .map_err(|error| {
   514                  format!("prepared-runtime public-aperture process session open failed: {error}")
   515              })?;
   516          let process_session_start_authority =
   517              Self::admit_process_session_start_with_live_session_for_direct_run_owner_v1(
   518                  kernel_state,
   519                  live_session,
   520                  aperture_label,
   521              )
   522              .map_err(DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1::ProcessSessionStartAdmission)?;
   523          let output =
   524              drive_process_session_start_with_typed_drive_authority_and_owner_execution_substrate(
   525                  process_session_start_authority,
   526              )?;
   527          finish_direct_run_public_aperture_drive_output(output)
   528              .map_err(DirectRunProcessSessionDriveFaultV1::Generic)
   529      }
   530  }

   491      pub fn commit_ready_into_session_execution_kernel_and_drive_to_direct_run_result_product_v1(
   492          self,
   493          session: &mut crate::ProcessSessionV0,
   494          boundary_context: &'static str,
   495      ) -> Result<
   496          (
   497              crate::DirectRunProcessSessionRunResultProductV1,
   498              ProviderDriveOutputEffectDrainReceiptBundleForProviderHostOwner,
   499          ),
   500          ProviderDriveSessionExecutionCommitFault,
   501      > {
   502          match self.payload {
   503              ProviderDriveResultPayload::Ready { result } => {
   504                  let (ready_output, output_effect_drain_receipts, host_resource_releases) = result
   505                      .into_output_for_provider_drive_result_owner_v1()
   506                      .into_ready_boundary_output_effect_drain_receipts_and_host_resource_releases_for_provider_drive_result_owner_v1();
   507                  let result_product = session
   508                      .commit_provider_ready_boundary_output_and_drive_to_direct_run_result_product_for_direct_run_owner_v1(
   509                          ready_output,
   510                          host_resource_releases,
   511                          boundary_context,
   512                      )?;
   513                  Ok((result_product, output_effect_drain_receipts))
   514              }
   515              ProviderDriveResultPayload::Parked { settlement: _ } => {
   516                  Err(ProviderDriveSessionExecutionCommitFault::NotReady)
   517              }
   518          }
   519      }

   148  impl PendingExecutableProviderBoundary {
   149      pub(in crate::session) fn pending_activity_for_observation(
   150          &self,
   151      ) -> &crate::session::work_runtime::PendingActivityEffectFrame {
   152          &self.pending_activity
   153      }
   154
   155      pub(in crate::session) fn consume_corresponded_ready_output(
   156          self,
   157          ready_output: swarm_capability_model::ProviderReadyBoundaryOutput,
   158          host_resource_releases: swarm_rust_sdk_static_provider_host::ProviderHostResourceReleaseTransferSetV1,
   159          runtime_heap: &mut crate::SessionRuntimeHeapOwner,
   160          execution_state: &mut crate::session::execution_state::KernelExecutionState,
   161          cursor: &mut ExecutableCursor,
   162      ) -> Result<(), crate::ProviderBoundaryIngressFault> {
   163          let Self {
   164              site: _,
   165              pending_activity: _,
   166              pending_output_authority,
   167              result_mode,
   168              result_commit,
   169              continuation,
   170              observable_effect_obligation: _observable_effect_obligation,
   171          } = self;
   172          let provider_output = pending_output_authority
   173              .consume_corresponded_ready_output_for_provider_boundary_owner_v1(ready_output)
   174              .map_err(crate::ProviderBoundaryIngressFault::from)?;
   175          let runtime_output = provider_output
   176              .try_map_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1(|provider_output| {
   177                  runtime_heap
   178                      .admit_corresponded_provider_output_transactionally_for_session_execution_kernel_owner_v1(
   179                          provider_output,
   180                          host_resource_releases,
   181                      )
   182              })
   183              .map_err(crate::ProviderBoundaryIngressFault::from)?;
   184          let settlement = crate::session::result_carrier_owner::SessionRuntimeProviderBoundarySettlement::from_correlated_provider_boundary_output(
   185              runtime_output,
   186          );
   187          execution_state
   188              .commit_corresponded_provider_effect_settlement(
   189                  runtime_heap,
   190                  result_mode,
   191                  result_commit,
   192                  settlement,
   193              )
   194              .map_err(crate::ProviderBoundaryExecutionCommitFault::from_provider_execution_frame)?;
   195          cursor.commit_target(&continuation);
   196          Ok(())
   197      }

   560      pub(crate) fn take_selected_provider_resume_host_input_for_direct_run_owner_v1(
   561          &mut self,
   562          effect_ref: &EffectRef,
   563      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderResumeHostInputForDirectRunOwnerV1, String> {
   564          let preflighted_effect = self
   565              .effects
   566              .preflight_provider_resume_host_input_for_effect_ref(
   567                  effect_ref,
   568                  "selected_provider_resume_host_input",
   569              )?;
   570          let preflighted_work =
   571              self.preflight_provider_resume_host_input_work_frame(effect_ref, &preflighted_effect)?;
   572          let preflighted_payload = self
   573              .payloads
   574              .preflight_activity_input_payload_for_provider_resume_host_input(
   575                  &preflighted_effect.input,
   576                  "selected_provider_resume_host_input",
   577              )?;
   578
   579          let invocation_authority = self
   580              .effects
   581              .consume_preflighted_provider_resume_host_input_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(
   582                  preflighted_effect,
   583                  "selected_provider_resume_host_input_commit",
   584              )?;
   585          let work_record = self
   586              .work
   587              .records
   588              .get_mut(&preflighted_work.work_id)
   589              .expect("provider-resume work preflight proved the exact record exists");
   590          let WorkFrame::ProviderResume(frame) = &mut work_record.frame else {
   591              unreachable!("provider-resume work preflight proved the exact frame kind")
   592          };
   593          let ProviderResumeSelectedAuthorityCustodyForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1::Pending {
   594              selected_contract,
   595              selected_output_authority,
   596              exact_static_child_use,
   597          } = std::mem::replace(
   598              &mut frame.selected_authority_custody,
   599              ProviderResumeSelectedAuthorityCustodyForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1::Consumed,
   600          )
   601          else {
   602              unreachable!("provider-resume work preflight proved pending selected custody")
   603          };
   604          let provider_input = self
   605              .payloads
   606              .take_preflighted_activity_input_payload_for_provider_resume_host_input(
   607                  preflighted_payload,
   608              );
   609          Ok(
   610              SelectedProviderResumeHostInputForDirectRunOwnerV1::from_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(
   611                  provider_input,
   612                  selected_contract,
   613                  selected_output_authority,
   614                  invocation_authority,
   615                  exact_static_child_use,
   616              ),
   617          )
   618      }

  1332      pub fn commit_selected_host_boundary_pending_activity_for_swarmvm_session_runtime_owner_v1(
  1333          &mut self,
  1334          activity_attempt_id: ActivityAttemptId,
  1335          provider_host_contract:
  1336              crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::CompilerExactProviderMaterializedCommandContractForSessionRuntimeOwnerV1,
  1337          payload: SessionRuntimeMaterializedActivityInputPayloadProduct,
  1338          exact_static_child_use: crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::SelectedProviderBoundaryExactStaticChildUseForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1,
  1339      ) -> Result<
  1340          (
  1341              PendingActivityEffectFrame,
  1342              PendingActivityEffectFrame,
  1343              swarm_capability_model::PendingProviderBoundaryOutputCommitAuthority,
  1344          ),
  1345          String,
  1346      > {
  1347          let effect_handle =
  1348              EffectHandle::host_activity_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(&activity_attempt_id);
  1349          if self.effects.contains_handle(&effect_handle) {
  1350              return Err(serde_json::json!({
  1351                  "kind": "selected_host_boundary_effect_duplicate_forbidden",
  1352                  "reason": "selected host-boundary admission may mint exactly one HostActivity effect record per activity attempt",
  1353                  "effect": "session_work_runtime_owned",
  1354              })
  1355              .to_string());
  1356          }
  1357          let effect_ref = effect_ref_from_handle(&effect_handle);
  1358          let resume_work_handle = WorkHandle::provider_resume(&activity_attempt_id);
  1359          let work_id = resume_work_handle.id.clone();
  1360          if self.work.records.contains_key(&work_id) {
  1361              return Err(serde_json::json!({
  1362                  "kind": "selected_host_boundary_provider_resume_work_duplicate_forbidden",
  1363                  "reason": "selected host-boundary admission may mint exactly one ProviderResume work record per activity attempt",
  1364                  "work_id": work_id.as_str(),
  1365              })
  1366              .to_string());
  1367          }
  1368          let (pending_output_authority, selected_output_authority) =
  1369              swarm_capability_model::mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
  1370          let input_handle = self.insert_activity_input_payload(&activity_attempt_id, payload)?;
  1371          let resume_frame = EffectResumeFrame {
  1372              activity_attempt_id: activity_attempt_id.duplicate_for_isa_owner(),
  1373              owner_actor_request_id: None,
  1374              owner_actor_delivery_id: None,
  1375              owner_actor_delivery_sequence: None,
  1376              owner_actor_request_context: None,
  1377              owner_actor_turn_id: None,
  1378          };
  1379          let descriptor = EffectDescriptor {
  1380              effect_ref: effect_ref.duplicate_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(),
  1381              kind: EffectKind::HostActivity,
  1382              operation_id: "session_work_runtime_owned".to_owned(),
  1383              site_id: "session_work_runtime_owned".to_owned(),
  1384              input: PayloadDescriptor::new(
  1385                  input_handle.duplicate_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(),
  1386                  PayloadSizeClass::ForbiddenInPublicAperture,
  1387                  None,
  1388              ),
  1389              resume_work: WorkRef::from_handle(&resume_work_handle),
  1390              deadline: None,
  1391          };
  1392          let record = EffectRecord::host_activity_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(
  1393              effect_handle.duplicate_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(),
  1394              input_handle,
  1395              resume_work_handle.duplicate_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(),
  1396              resume_frame,
  1397              descriptor,
  1398              None,
  1399              None,
  1400          );
  1401          self.effects.insert_record(record)?;
  1402          self.work.records.insert(
  1403              work_id,
  1404              WorkRecord {
  1405                  handle: resume_work_handle.duplicate_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(),
  1406                  prepared: None,
  1407                  authority: WorkAuthority {
  1408                      authority_kind: "selected_host_boundary_provider_resume".to_owned(),
  1409                  },
  1410                  frame: WorkFrame::ProviderResume(ProviderResumeWorkFrame {
  1411                      effect: effect_handle.duplicate_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(),
  1412                      selected_authority_custody:
  1413                          ProviderResumeSelectedAuthorityCustodyForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1::Pending {
  1414                              selected_contract: provider_host_contract,
  1415                              selected_output_authority,
  1416                              exact_static_child_use,
  1417                          },
  1418                  }),
  1419                  retention: WorkRetentionClass::EffectResume,
  1420                  status: WorkStatus::WaitingOnEffect,
  1421                  created_by: WorkCreationCause {
  1422                      cause_kind: "selected_host_boundary_pending_activity".to_owned(),
  1423                  },
  1424              },
  1425          );
  1426          self.scheduler
  1427              .blocking_provider
  1428              .enqueue_provider_admission_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(
  1429                  resume_work_handle.duplicate_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(),
  1430              );
  1431          let pending_activity = self.effects.require_pending_activity_frame(
  1432              &effect_ref,
  1433              "selected_host_boundary_pending_activity_admission",
  1434          )?;
  1435          let pending_effect = pending_activity.duplicate_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1();
  1436          Ok((pending_activity, pending_effect, pending_output_authority))
  1437      }

   252          provider_input: ProviderValue,

   254          selected_output_authority: SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority,
   255          invocation_authority: SelectedProviderResumeInvocationAuthorityForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1,

   266          }
   267      }
   268
   269      /// Observation-only negative prefilter. A positive result grants no route:
   270      /// the caller must still consume `self` through the exact catalogue identity
   271      /// and operation transition below. This avoids materializing the builtin
   272      /// catalogue for unrelated provider calls.
   273      pub fn contract_package_export_matches_for_direct_run_routing_observation_v1(
   274          &self,
   275          package_specifier: &str,
   276          export_name: &str,
   277      ) -> bool {
   278          self.admitted_contract.identity().package_specifier() == package_specifier
   279              && self.admitted_contract.identity().export_name() == export_name
   280      }
   281
   282      /// Select one kernel-internal command only from an exact sealed catalogue
   283      /// identity and its exact Contract-TSON command operation. A different
   284      /// identity preserves the complete request for the ordinary provider-host
   285      /// route; an operation mismatch on the exact identity refuses typed.
   286      pub(crate) fn select_exact_kernel_internal_command_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   287          self,
   288          expected_identity: &swarm_capability_model::CapabilityContractIdentity,
   289          expected_operation: &str,
   290      ) -> Result<SelectedProviderResumeRouteForDirectRunOwnerV1, String> {
   291          let Self {
   292              provider_input,
   293              admitted_contract,
   294              selected_output_authority,
   295              invocation_authority,
   296              exact_static_child_use,
   297              _seal: _,
   298          } = self;
   299          match admitted_contract
   300              .select_exact_kernel_internal_command_for_contract_tson_owner_v1(
   301                  expected_identity,
   302                  expected_operation,
   303              )
   304              .map_err(|fault| fault.to_string())?
   305          {
   306              swarm_capability_contract_tson::ExactKernelInternalCommandContractSelectionForContractTsonOwnerV1::Exact(
   307                  exact_contract,
   308              ) => {
   309                  let selected_input = SelectedKernelInternalProviderResumeInputForDirectRunOwnerV1 {
   310                      provider_input,
   311                      invocation_authority,
   312                      output_settlement_authority:
   313                          SelectedKernelInternalProviderOutputSettlementAuthorityForDirectRunOwnerV1 {
   314                              exact_contract,
   315                              selected_output_authority,
   316                              _seal: selected_kernel_internal_provider_output_settlement_private::Seal,
   317                          },
   318                      _seal: selected_kernel_internal_provider_resume_input_private::Seal,
   319                  };
   320                  Ok(match exact_static_child_use {
   321                      crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::SelectedProviderBoundaryExactStaticChildUseForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1::Ordinary => {
   322                          SelectedProviderResumeRouteForDirectRunOwnerV1::KernelInternal(
   323                              selected_input,
   324                          )
   325                      }
   326                      crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::SelectedProviderBoundaryExactStaticChildUseForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1::Exact(
   327                          crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::PreparedBoundProviderExactStaticChildUseAuthorityV1::ProcessLoad(
   328                              exact_use_authority,
   329                          ),
   330                      ) => SelectedProviderResumeRouteForDirectRunOwnerV1::ProcessLoad(
   331                          SelectedProcessLoadExactStaticChildProviderResumeInputForDirectRunOwnerV1::from_selected_exact_use_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(
   332                              selected_input,
   333                              exact_use_authority,
   334                          ),
   335                      ),
   336                      crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::SelectedProviderBoundaryExactStaticChildUseForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1::Exact(
   337                          crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::PreparedBoundProviderExactStaticChildUseAuthorityV1::ProcessRestore(
   338                              exact_use_authority,
   339                          ),
   340                      ) => SelectedProviderResumeRouteForDirectRunOwnerV1::ProcessRestore(
   341                          SelectedProcessRestoreProviderResumeInputForDirectRunOwnerV1::from_selected_exact_use_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(
   342                              selected_input,
   343                              exact_use_authority,
   344                          ),
   345                      ),
   346                      crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::SelectedProviderBoundaryExactStaticChildUseForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1::Exact(
   347                          crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::PreparedBoundProviderExactStaticChildUseAuthorityV1::ProcessRun(
   348                              exact_use_authority,
   349                          ),
   350                      ) => SelectedProviderResumeRouteForDirectRunOwnerV1::ProcessRun(
   351                          SelectedProcessRunExactStaticChildProviderResumeInputForDirectRunOwnerV1::from_selected_exact_use_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(
   352                              selected_input,
   353                              exact_use_authority,
   354                          ),
   355                      ),
   356                      crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::SelectedProviderBoundaryExactStaticChildUseForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1::Exact(
   357                          crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::PreparedBoundProviderExactStaticChildUseAuthorityV1::ProcessInvoke(
   358                              exact_use_authority,
   359                          ),
   360                      ) => SelectedProviderResumeRouteForDirectRunOwnerV1::ProcessInvoke(
   361                          SelectedProcessInvokeExactStaticChildProviderResumeInputForDirectRunOwnerV1::from_selected_exact_use_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1(
   362                              selected_input,
   363                              exact_use_authority,
   364                          ),
   365                      ),
   366                  })
   367              }
   368              swarm_capability_contract_tson::ExactKernelInternalCommandContractSelectionForContractTsonOwnerV1::DifferentContract(
   369                  admitted_contract,
   370              ) => Ok(SelectedProviderResumeRouteForDirectRunOwnerV1::ProviderHost(Self {
   371                  provider_input,
   372                  admitted_contract,
   373                  selected_output_authority,

   381      fn into_contract_and_provider_input_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1(

   389              provider_input,
   390              admitted_contract,
   391              selected_output_authority,
   392              invocation_authority,
   393              exact_static_child_use,
   394              _seal: _,
   395          } = self;
   396          let crate::session::execution_kernel::executable_image::SelectedProviderBoundaryExactStaticChildUseForSessionWorkRuntimeOwnerV1::Ordinary = exact_static_child_use else {
   397              unreachable!(
   398                  "exact static-child ProviderResume custody must select its Load/Run/Invoke kernel route"
   399              )
   400          };
   401          invocation_authority.consume_for_provider_host_route_for_session_work_runtime_owner_v1();
   402          (admitted_contract, provider_input, selected_output_authority)
   403      }
   404
   405      /// One-shot admission of the selected ProviderResume host input into the
   406      /// host-admitted typed request. Routing between the static provider host
   407      /// map and the kernel-owned product-session event route happens at the
   408      /// direct-run drive, which consumes the declared product-session boundary
   409      /// predicate over this admitted request.
   410      pub fn admit_host_typed_request_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   411          self,
   412          provider_execution_session: &swarm_provider_host_set::ProviderHostExecutionSession,
   413      ) -> swarm_capability_model::CapabilitySdkResult<SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest> {
   414          let (contract, provider_input, selected_output_authority) =
   415              self.into_contract_and_provider_input_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1();
   416          provider_execution_session
   417              .admit_selected_boundary_typed_request_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1(

    23  pub struct SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority {
    24      seal: Arc<ProviderBoundaryCorrespondenceSeal>,
    25  }
    26
    27  /// Successful correspondence between the session-owned pending half and the
    28  /// selected kernel-internal half of one provider boundary.
    29  ///
    30  /// This product deliberately carries no provider value. Kernel-internal

    33  /// correspondence.

    35  #[must_use = "a matched provider-boundary output authority must be consumed by the nominal output owner"]
    36  pub struct MatchedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority {
    37      _seal: Arc<ProviderBoundaryCorrespondenceSeal>,
    38  }
    39
    40  /// Non-destructive join for a kernel-internal nominal output. A mismatch
    41  /// returns both complete halves so the pending session boundary and selected
    42  /// command output can be restored and retried without reconstructing a key.
    43  pub enum ProviderBoundaryOutputAuthorityJoin {
    44      Joined(MatchedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority),
    45      Unmatched {
    46          pending: PendingProviderBoundaryOutputCommitAuthority,
    47          selected: SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority,
    48      },
    49  }
    50
    51  /// Authored provider cargo paired with the exact selected host request that
    52  /// produced it.  Its cargo is unavailable until the session consumes the
    53  /// matching pending half.
    54  #[must_use = "a ready provider-boundary output must be consumed by its exact pending session application"]
    55  pub struct ProviderReadyBoundaryOutput {
    56      seal: Arc<ProviderBoundaryCorrespondenceSeal>,
    57      output: ProviderReadyBoundaryOutputInner,
    58  }
    59
    60  enum ProviderReadyBoundaryOutputInner {
    61      Authored(ProviderValue),
    62      Accepted(ProviderValue),
    63      Rejected(ProviderValue),
    64  }
    65
    66  /// One corresponded provider output whose settlement meaning remains sealed
    67  /// while its authored payload crosses into the session-runtime value domain.
    68  ///
    69  /// The payload mapper cannot select or alter the settlement branch.  The
    70  /// correspondence owner applies it exactly once to the payload already paired
    71  /// with the selected provider boundary.
    72  #[must_use = "a corresponded provider output must be consumed into authored cargo or a typed result settlement"]
    73  pub struct CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutput<T> {
    74      inner: CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner<T>,
    75  }
    76
    77  enum CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner<T> {
    78      Authored(T),
    79      Accepted(T),
    80      Rejected(T),
    81  }
    82
    83  /// Ordinary authored provider cargo.  Unlike a typed command settlement, its
    84  /// value may still contain a language-level Result envelope that only the
    85  /// session runtime can classify.
    86  #[must_use = "corresponded authored provider cargo must enter the session runtime exactly once"]
    87  pub struct CorrelatedAuthoredProviderOutput<T> {
    88      payload: T,
    89  }
    90
    91  /// A provider-host-admitted command settlement.  Its accepted/rejected branch
    92  /// is private and can only be consumed through the finite one-shot settlement
    93  /// operation below.
    94  #[must_use = "a typed provider settlement must be consumed into one session-runtime Result carrier"]
    95  pub struct CorrelatedProviderResultSettlement<T> {
    96      inner: CorrelatedProviderResultSettlementInner<T>,
    97  }
    98
    99  enum CorrelatedProviderResultSettlementInner<T> {
   100      Accepted(T),
   101      Rejected(T),
   102  }
   103
   104  /// The rejected half returned by the finite accepted-settlement consume.  It
   105  /// is non-constructible and retains its payload until the session Result owner
   106  /// consumes it into an Err carrier.
   107  #[must_use = "a rejected provider settlement must be consumed into the session-runtime Err carrier"]
   108  pub struct CorrelatedRejectedProviderResultSettlement<T> {
   109      payload: T,
   110  }
   111
   112  struct ProviderBoundaryCorrespondenceSeal;
   113
   114  #[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Error)]
   115  pub enum ProviderBoundaryOutputCorrespondenceFault {
   116      #[error("ready provider output does not belong to the selected pending provider boundary")]
   117      ReadyOutputDoesNotMatchPendingBoundary,
   118  }
   119
   120  /// Mint the two linear halves of exactly one selected provider boundary.
   121  ///
   122  /// The halves alone grant no execution authority: the pending half can only
   123  /// enter a session through the private selected-boundary commit, and the host
   124  /// half can only become useful after moving through the admitted request into
   125  /// a ready output.  The shared seal is never projected as a raw id.
   126  pub fn mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1() -> (
   127      PendingProviderBoundaryOutputCommitAuthority,
   128      SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority,
   129  ) {
   130      let seal = Arc::new(ProviderBoundaryCorrespondenceSeal);
   131      (
   132          PendingProviderBoundaryOutputCommitAuthority {
   133              seal: Arc::clone(&seal),
   134          },
   135          SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority { seal },
   136      )
   137  }
   138
   139  impl SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority {
   140      /// Consume the selected-host half into the single ready output admitted by
   141      /// that host request.  ProviderValue is authored cargo; it cannot be read
   142      /// back until the paired pending session half is also consumed.
   143      pub fn admit_ready_output_for_provider_host_owner_v1(
   144          self,
   145          output: ProviderValue,
   146      ) -> ProviderReadyBoundaryOutput {
   147          ProviderReadyBoundaryOutput {
   148              seal: self.seal,
   149              output: ProviderReadyBoundaryOutputInner::Authored(output),
   150          }
   151      }
   152
   153      /// Consume the selected-host half into a typed successful command
   154      /// settlement.  The provider host may call this only after admitting the
   155      /// exact operation invocation result; no result tag or object shape is
   156      /// reconstructed by the session.
   157      pub fn admit_accepted_result_for_provider_host_owner_v1(
   158          self,
   159          output: ProviderValue,
   160      ) -> ProviderReadyBoundaryOutput {
   161          ProviderReadyBoundaryOutput {
   162              seal: self.seal,
   163              output: ProviderReadyBoundaryOutputInner::Accepted(output),
   164          }
   165      }
   166
   167      /// Consume the selected-host half into a typed rejected command
   168      /// settlement.  Runtime/transport/admission faults do not use this path;
   169      /// it is only for an authored rejection admitted by the provider host.
   170      pub fn admit_rejected_result_for_provider_host_owner_v1(
   171          self,
   172          output: ProviderValue,
   173      ) -> ProviderReadyBoundaryOutput {
   174          ProviderReadyBoundaryOutput {
   175              seal: self.seal,
   176              output: ProviderReadyBoundaryOutputInner::Rejected(output),
   177          }
   178      }
   179  }
   180
   181  impl PendingProviderBoundaryOutputCommitAuthority {
   182      /// Join the pending session half directly to the selected half retained by
   183      /// an exact kernel-internal command. This is the nominal-output sibling of
   184      /// `consume_corresponded_ready_output_for_provider_boundary_owner_v1`.
   185      pub fn try_join_selected_output_authority_for_kernel_internal_owner_v1(
   186          self,
   187          selected: SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority,
   188      ) -> ProviderBoundaryOutputAuthorityJoin {
   189          if Arc::ptr_eq(&self.seal, &selected.seal) {
   190              ProviderBoundaryOutputAuthorityJoin::Joined(MatchedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority {
   191                  _seal: self.seal,
   192              })
   193          } else {
   194              ProviderBoundaryOutputAuthorityJoin::Unmatched {
   195                  pending: self,
   196                  selected,
   197              }
   198          }
   199      }
   200
   201      /// Consume both halves and release authored cargo only when they came from
   202      /// the same mint.  The kernel calls this inside its finite provider-ready
   203      /// commit; no route, register, activity id, or fingerprint is reconstructed.
   204      pub fn consume_corresponded_ready_output_for_provider_boundary_owner_v1(
   205          self,
   206          ready: ProviderReadyBoundaryOutput,
   207      ) -> Result<
   208          CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutput<ProviderValue>,
   209          ProviderBoundaryOutputCorrespondenceFault,
   210      > {
   211          if !Arc::ptr_eq(&self.seal, &ready.seal) {
   212              return Err(
   213                  ProviderBoundaryOutputCorrespondenceFault::ReadyOutputDoesNotMatchPendingBoundary,
   214              );
   215          }
   216          let inner = match ready.output {
   217              ProviderReadyBoundaryOutputInner::Authored(payload) => {
   218                  CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Authored(payload)
   219              }
   220              ProviderReadyBoundaryOutputInner::Accepted(payload) => {
   221                  CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Accepted(payload)
   222              }
   223              ProviderReadyBoundaryOutputInner::Rejected(payload) => {
   224                  CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Rejected(payload)
   225              }
   226          };
   227          Ok(CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutput { inner })
   228      }
   229  }
   230
   231  impl<T> CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutput<T> {
   232      /// Map only the authored payload while preserving the private settlement
   233      /// branch byte-for-byte.  The mapper is invoked once and cannot choose,
   234      /// forge, or substitute Accepted/Rejected authority.
   235      pub fn try_map_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1<U, E>(
   236          self,
   237          map: impl FnOnce(T) -> Result<U, E>,
   238      ) -> Result<CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutput<U>, E> {
   239          let inner = match self.inner {
   240              CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Authored(payload) => {
   241                  CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Authored(map(payload)?)
   242              }
   243              CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Accepted(payload) => {
   244                  CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Accepted(map(payload)?)
   245              }
   246              CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Rejected(payload) => {
   247                  CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Rejected(map(payload)?)
   248              }
   249          };
   250          Ok(CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutput { inner })
   251      }
   252
   253      /// Separate ordinary authored cargo from a provider-host-admitted command
   254      /// settlement without exposing a public discriminant or result tag.
   255      pub fn into_result_settlement_for_session_runtime_owner_v1(
   256          self,
   257      ) -> Result<CorrelatedProviderResultSettlement<T>, CorrelatedAuthoredProviderOutput<T>> {
   258          match self.inner {
   259              CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Authored(payload) => {
   260                  Err(CorrelatedAuthoredProviderOutput { payload })
   261              }
   262              CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Accepted(payload) => {
   263                  Ok(CorrelatedProviderResultSettlement {
   264                      inner: CorrelatedProviderResultSettlementInner::Accepted(payload),
   265                  })
   266              }
   267              CorrelatedProviderBoundaryOutputInner::Rejected(payload) => {
   268                  Ok(CorrelatedProviderResultSettlement {
   269                      inner: CorrelatedProviderResultSettlementInner::Rejected(payload),
   270                  })
   271              }
   272          }
   273      }
   274  }
   275
   276  impl<T> CorrelatedAuthoredProviderOutput<T> {
   277      pub fn into_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1(self) -> T {
   278          self.payload
   279      }
   280  }
   281
   282  impl<T> CorrelatedProviderResultSettlement<T> {
   283      /// Consume one typed settlement.  Accepted releases its payload; rejected
   284      /// remains sealed in a distinct one-shot product for the Err owner.
   285      pub fn into_accepted_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1(
   286          self,
   287      ) -> Result<T, CorrelatedRejectedProviderResultSettlement<T>> {
   288          match self.inner {
   289              CorrelatedProviderResultSettlementInner::Accepted(payload) => Ok(payload),
   290              CorrelatedProviderResultSettlementInner::Rejected(payload) => {
   291                  Err(CorrelatedRejectedProviderResultSettlement { payload })
   292              }
   293          }
   294      }
   295  }
   296
   297  impl<T> CorrelatedRejectedProviderResultSettlement<T> {
   298      pub fn into_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1(self) -> T {
   299          self.payload
   300      }
   301  }

   363  #[cfg(test)]
   364  mod tests {
   365      use super::*;
   366
   367      #[test]
   368      fn exact_pair_releases_authored_output_once() {
   369          let (pending, selected) = mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
   370          let ready = selected.admit_ready_output_for_provider_host_owner_v1(ProviderValue::String(
   371              "ready".to_owned(),
   372          ));
   373
   374          let correlated = pending
   375              .consume_corresponded_ready_output_for_provider_boundary_owner_v1(ready)
   376              .expect("the paired ready output must commit");
   377          let authored = correlated
   378              .into_result_settlement_for_session_runtime_owner_v1()
   379              .expect_err("ordinary authored cargo must not become a typed settlement");
   380          assert_eq!(
   381              authored.into_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1(),
   382              ProviderValue::String("ready".to_owned())
   383          );
   384      }
   385
   386      #[test]
   387      fn exact_pair_releases_typed_accepted_settlement_once() {
   388          let (pending, selected) = mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
   389          let ready = selected.admit_accepted_result_for_provider_host_owner_v1(
   390              ProviderValue::String("accepted".to_owned()),
   391          );
   392          let correlated = pending
   393              .consume_corresponded_ready_output_for_provider_boundary_owner_v1(ready)
   394              .expect("the paired accepted result must correspond")
   395              .try_map_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1(|payload| {
   396                  Ok::<_, ()>(match payload {
   397                      ProviderValue::String(text) => text.len(),
   398                      _ => panic!("test payload must remain the admitted string"),
   399                  })
   400              })
   401              .expect("payload mapping must succeed");
   402          let settlement = correlated
   403              .into_result_settlement_for_session_runtime_owner_v1()
   404              .expect("accepted result must remain a typed settlement");
   405          assert_eq!(
   406              settlement
   407                  .into_accepted_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1()
   408                  .expect("accepted result must release its payload"),
   409              "accepted".len()
   410          );
   411      }
   412
   413      #[test]
   414      fn exact_pair_releases_typed_rejected_settlement_once() {
   415          let (pending, selected) = mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
   416          let ready = selected.admit_rejected_result_for_provider_host_owner_v1(
   417              ProviderValue::String("rejected".to_owned()),
   418          );
   419          let settlement = pending
   420              .consume_corresponded_ready_output_for_provider_boundary_owner_v1(ready)
   421              .expect("the paired rejected result must correspond")
   422              .into_result_settlement_for_session_runtime_owner_v1()
   423              .expect("rejected result must remain a typed settlement");
   424          let rejected = settlement
   425              .into_accepted_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1()
   426              .expect_err("rejected result must not release an accepted payload");
   427          assert_eq!(
   428              rejected.into_payload_for_session_runtime_owner_v1(),
   429              ProviderValue::String("rejected".to_owned())
   430          );
   431      }
   432
   433      #[test]
   434      fn cross_boundary_swap_is_a_typed_fault() {
   435          let (left_pending, _left_selected) = mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
   436          let (_right_pending, right_selected) = mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
   437          let swapped = right_selected.admit_ready_output_for_provider_host_owner_v1(
   438              ProviderValue::String("wrong boundary".to_owned()),
   439          );
   440
   441          assert_eq!(
   442              left_pending
   443                  .consume_corresponded_ready_output_for_provider_boundary_owner_v1(swapped)
   444                  .expect_err("a ready output from another boundary must be refused"),
   445              ProviderBoundaryOutputCorrespondenceFault::ReadyOutputDoesNotMatchPendingBoundary
   446          );
   447      }
   448
   449      #[test]
   450      fn cross_boundary_swap_preserves_typed_settlement_refusal() {
   451          let (left_pending, _left_selected) = mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
   452          let (_right_pending, right_selected) = mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
   453          let swapped = right_selected.admit_accepted_result_for_provider_host_owner_v1(
   454              ProviderValue::String("wrong accepted boundary".to_owned()),
   455          );
   456
   457          assert_eq!(
   458              left_pending
   459                  .consume_corresponded_ready_output_for_provider_boundary_owner_v1(swapped)
   460                  .expect_err("a typed settlement from another boundary must be refused"),
   461              ProviderBoundaryOutputCorrespondenceFault::ReadyOutputDoesNotMatchPendingBoundary
   462          );
   463      }
   464
   465      #[test]
   466      fn nominal_join_preserves_both_halves_on_mismatch() {
   467          let (left_pending, _left_selected) = mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
   468          let (_right_pending, right_selected) = mint_provider_boundary_output_correspondence_v1();
   469
   470          let ProviderBoundaryOutputAuthorityJoin::Unmatched { pending, selected } = left_pending
   471              .try_join_selected_output_authority_for_kernel_internal_owner_v1(right_selected)
   472          else {
   473              panic!("independently minted provider boundaries must not join");
   474          };
   475
   476          assert!(matches!(
   477              pending.try_join_selected_output_authority_for_kernel_internal_owner_v1(selected),
   478              ProviderBoundaryOutputAuthorityJoin::Unmatched { .. }
   479          ));
   480      }
   481  }

   100      pub fn observation(&self) -> ProviderHostSetObservation {
   101          let observation = ProviderHostSetObservation::external_transport_for_provider_host_owner_v1(
   102              self.host_id().to_owned(),
   103              self.host_kind().to_owned(),
   104          );
   105          debug_assert_eq!(observation.host_id(), self.host_id());
   106          debug_assert_eq!(observation.host_kind(), self.host_kind());
   107          debug_assert_eq!(
   108              observation.provider_execution_domain(),
   109              ProviderExecutionDomain::ExternalTransportCapabilityProvider.as_str()
   110          );
   111          observation
   112      }
   113  }
   114
   115  impl ExternalTransportCapabilityProviderExecutionSession {
   116      pub(crate) fn invoke_manifest_resolved_call_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(
   117          &mut self,
   118          invocation: DurableExternalProviderInvocationAuthority,
   119      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner> {
   120          if self.provider.is_none() {
   121              self.provider = Some(self.factory.open_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1()?);
   122          }
   123          self.provider
   124              .as_mut()
   125              .expect("external provider is installed after lazy open")
   126              .invoke_manifest_resolved_call_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(invocation)
   127      }
   128
   129      pub(crate) fn shutdown_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(&mut self) -> CapabilitySdkResult<()> {
   130          if let Some(provider) = self.provider.as_mut() {
   131              provider.shutdown_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1()?;
   132          }
   133          self.provider = None;
   134          Ok(())
   135      }
   136  }

   899      pub fn admit_selected_boundary_typed_request_for_direct_run_provider_resume_owner_v1(
   900          &self,
   901          contract: AdmittedCapabilityContractTson,
   902          input: ProviderValue,
   903          selected_output_authority: SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority,
   904      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest> {
   905          let provider_imports = self
   906              .provider_import_execution_start_admission
   907              .as_ref()
   908              .ok_or_else(|| {
   909                  CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(
   910                      "selected provider-boundary admission requires the prepared-runtime provider-import execution-start admission first"
   911                          .to_owned(),
   912                  )
   913              })?;
   914          match provider_imports
   915              .select_exact_external_call_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(contract)
   916              .map_err(|fault| {
   917                  CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(fault.to_string())
   918              })? {
   919              ManifestResolvedExternalProviderCallAdmissionSelection::Admitted(admission) => {
   920                  SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest::from_manifest_resolved_external_call_admission_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(
   921                      admission,
   922                      input,

   924                  )
   925              }
   926              ManifestResolvedExternalProviderCallAdmissionSelection::Unmatched(contract) => Ok(self
   927                  .provider_hosts
   928                  .admit_typed_request_for_provider_host_set_owner_contract_tson_v1(contract, input)?
   929                  .into_selected_provider_boundary_request_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(
   930                      selected_output_authority,
   931                  )),
   932          }
   933      }

   935      /// Classify + admit a loaded-native provider call at the kernel drive. The
   936      /// session already wraps the full [`ProviderHostSet`] (the kernel holds it
   937      /// across the drive); this is the existing threaded consume — no new
   938      /// dependency edge. The kernel consumes the returned sealed classification to
   939      /// mint the route-descriptor-free park; the sealed dlopen handle stays in the
   940      /// loader (identity-admission at park, handle-resolution at source-advance).
   941      pub fn admit_loaded_native_park_typed_request_for_direct_run_kernel_owner_contract_tson_v1(

   948                  contract, input,
   949              )
   950      }

   952      pub fn drive_rust_sdk_static_provider_request_for_provider_host_set_owner_contract_tson_v1(
   953          &mut self,
   954          contract: AdmittedCapabilityContractTson,

   956      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<RustSdkStaticProviderExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner> {
   957          let request = self

   999          &mut self,
  1000          request: HostAdmittedTypedProviderRequest,

  1002      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<RustSdkStaticProviderExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner> {

  1007      pub fn invoke_selected_provider_boundary_request_for_direct_run_owner_v1(
  1008          &mut self,
  1009          request: SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest,
  1010      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner> {
  1011          let provider_id = request.provider_id().to_owned();
  1012          match request.into_route_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1() {
  1013              SelectedProviderBoundaryRequestRouteForProviderHostSetOwnerV1::RustSdk(request) => {

  1015                      .invoke_selected_provider_boundary_request_for_direct_run_owner_v1(request)
  1016              }
  1017              SelectedProviderBoundaryRequestRouteForProviderHostSetOwnerV1::ManifestResolvedExternal(
  1018                  invocation,

  1020                  .external_provider_session
  1021                  .as_mut()
  1022                  .ok_or_else(|| CapabilitySdkError::NoExternalCapabilityProviderHost {
  1023                      provider_id,
  1024                      provider_execution_domain:
  1025                          swarm_capability_model::EXTERNAL_TRANSPORT_PROVIDER_DOMAIN.to_owned(),
  1026                  })?
  1027                  .invoke_manifest_resolved_call_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(invocation),

    19          admissions: RustSdkStaticProviderHostAdmissionSet,
    20      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<Self> {

    29                      contract_families

   537              .into_request_and_selected_output_authority_for_static_provider_host_owner_v1()?;
   538          let context =
   539              ProviderHostContext::from_admitted_request_for_swarm_rust_sdk_static_provider_host_owner_v1(
   540                  &request,
   541              )?;
   542          let result = self.invoke_admitted_request(request, context)?;
   543          Ok(
   544              SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner::from_static_provider_host_owner_v1(
   545                  result,
   546                  selected_output_authority,
   547              ),
   548          )
   549      }
   550
   551      fn provider_binding_for_contract(
   552          &self,
   553          contract: &CapabilityContractIdentity,
   554      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<Option<RustSdkStaticProviderBinding>> {
   555          if let Some(provider) = self.exact_providers.iter().find(|provider| {
   556              provider
   557                  .binding
   558                  .matches_identity_for_static_provider_host_owner_v1(contract)
   559          }) {

   562                      .binding
   563                      .duplicate_for_static_provider_host_owner_v1(),

   565          }

  1177  impl fmt::Debug for SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest {
  1178      fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
  1179          let route = match &self.request {
  1180              SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::RustSdk(_) => "rust_sdk",
  1181              SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::ManifestResolvedExternal { .. } => {
  1182                  "manifest_resolved_external"
  1183              }
  1184          };
  1185          formatter
  1186              .debug_struct("SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest")
  1187              .field("route", &route)
  1188              .field("request", &"<sealed>")
  1189              .field("selected_output_authority", &"<sealed>")
  1190              .finish()
  1191      }
  1192  }
  1193
  1194  impl SelectedProviderBoundaryHostRequest {
  1195      pub fn from_manifest_resolved_external_call_admission_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(
  1196          admission: ManifestResolvedExternalProviderCallAdmission,
  1197          input: ProviderValue,
  1198          selected_output_authority: SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority,
  1199      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<Self> {
  1200          // The prepared-runtime owner already joined the private route operation
  1201          // to this Contract-TSON. Keep that proof whole across the public host-set
  1202          // aperture; splitting the two arguments there would permit a same-contract
  1203          // Contract-TSON for a different operation to be substituted here.
  1204          let (call_authority, contract) =
  1205              admission.into_call_authority_and_contract_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1();
  1206          let contract_identity = contract.identity().duplicate_for_capability_model_owner();
  1207          if call_authority.contract_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1() != &contract_identity {
  1208              return Err(CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(
  1209                  "manifest-resolved external call authority does not match selected Contract-TSON identity"
  1210                      .to_owned(),
  1211              ));
  1212          }
  1213          let provider_id = contract_identity.provider_id();
  1214          let (operation, output_type_contract) = contract
  1215              .into_operation_and_output_type_contract_authority_for_provider_host_owner_v1()
  1216              .map_err(|source| {
  1217                  CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(format!(
  1218                      "manifest-resolved external provider Contract-TSON operation admission failed: {source}"
  1219                  ))
  1220              })?;
  1221          Ok(Self {
  1222              request: SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::ManifestResolvedExternal {
  1223                  call_authority,
  1224                  request: TypedProviderRequest {
  1225                      provider_id,
  1226                      contract: contract_identity,
  1227                      operation,
  1228                      output_type_contract,
  1229                      input,
  1230                      invocation: None,
  1231                  },
  1232              },
  1233              selected_output_authority,
  1234          })
  1235      }
  1236
  1237      pub fn provider_id(&self) -> &str {
  1238          match &self.request {
  1239              SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::RustSdk(request) => request.provider_id(),
  1240              SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::ManifestResolvedExternal { request, .. } => {
  1241                  request.provider_id()
  1242              }
  1243          }
  1244      }
  1245
  1246      pub fn provider_input(&self) -> &ProviderValue {
  1247          match &self.request {
  1248              SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::RustSdk(request) => request.provider_input(),
  1249              SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::ManifestResolvedExternal { request, .. } => {
  1250                  request.input()
  1251              }
  1252          }
  1253      }
  1254
  1255      pub fn into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(
  1256          self,
  1257          output: ProviderValue,
  1258      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner> {
  1259          let Self {
  1260              request,
  1261              selected_output_authority,
  1262          } = self;
  1263          let SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::RustSdk(request) = request else {
  1264              return Err(CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(
  1265                  "manifest-resolved external request cannot be settled by a Rust SDK executor"
  1266                      .to_owned(),
  1267              ));
  1268          };
  1269          let result =
  1270              request.into_execution_result_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(output)?;
  1271          Ok(
  1272              SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner {
  1273                  result,
  1274                  selected_output_authority,
  1275              },
  1276          )
  1277      }
  1278
  1279      fn into_request_and_selected_output_authority_for_static_provider_host_owner_v1(
  1280          self,
  1281      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<(
  1282          HostAdmittedTypedProviderRequest,
  1283          SelectedProviderBoundaryOutputAuthority,
  1284      )> {
  1285          let SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::RustSdk(request) = self.request else {
  1286              return Err(CapabilitySdkError::InvalidDirectRunProviderRequirement(
  1287                  "manifest-resolved external request cannot enter the Rust SDK host set".to_owned(),
  1288              ));
  1289          };
  1290          Ok((request, self.selected_output_authority))
  1291      }
  1292
  1293      pub fn into_route_for_provider_host_set_owner_v1(
  1294          self,
  1295      ) -> SelectedProviderBoundaryRequestRouteForProviderHostSetOwnerV1 {
  1296          let Self {
  1297              request,
  1298              selected_output_authority,
  1299          } = self;
  1300          match request {
  1301              SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::RustSdk(request) => {
  1302                  SelectedProviderBoundaryRequestRouteForProviderHostSetOwnerV1::RustSdk(Self {
  1303                      request: SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::RustSdk(request),
  1304                      selected_output_authority,
  1305                  })
  1306              }
  1307              SelectedProviderBoundaryTypedRequest::ManifestResolvedExternal {
  1308                  call_authority,
  1309                  request,
  1310              } => {
  1311                  let TypedProviderRequest {
  1312                      provider_id,
  1313                      contract,
  1314                      operation: _,
  1315                      output_type_contract,
  1316                      input,
  1317                      invocation,
  1318                  } = request;
  1319                  SelectedProviderBoundaryRequestRouteForProviderHostSetOwnerV1::ManifestResolvedExternal(
  1320                      DurableExternalProviderInvocationAuthority {
  1321                          call_authority,
  1322                          provider_input: input,
  1323                          output_settlement: DurableExternalProviderOutputSettlementAuthority {
  1324                              request: TypedProviderOutputSettlementAuthority {
  1325                                  provider_id,
  1326                                  contract,
  1327                                  output_type_contract,
  1328                                  invocation,
  1329                              },
  1330                              selected_output_authority,
  1331                          },
  1332                      },
  1333                  )
  1334              }
  1335          }
  1336      }
  1337  }
  1338
  1339  impl DurableExternalProviderInvocationAuthority {
  1340      pub fn into_call_input_and_output_settlement_for_durable_external_provider_owner_v1(
  1341          self,
  1342      ) -> (
  1343          ManifestResolvedExternalProviderCallAuthority,
  1344          ProviderValue,
  1345          DurableExternalProviderOutputSettlementAuthority,
  1346      ) {
  1347          (
  1348              self.call_authority,
  1349              self.provider_input,
  1350              self.output_settlement,
  1351          )
  1352      }
  1353  }
  1354
  1355  impl DurableExternalProviderOutputSettlementAuthority {
  1356      pub fn settle_ready_for_durable_external_provider_owner_v1(
  1357          self,
  1358          output: ProviderValue,
  1359      ) -> CapabilitySdkResult<SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner> {
  1360          let ready_output = self
  1361              .request
  1362              .into_ready_output_for_provider_host_owner_v1(output, Vec::new())?;
  1363          let result = RustSdkStaticProviderExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner::ready_for_rust_sdk_static_provider_executor_owner_v1(
  1364              ready_output,
  1365              ProviderHostResourceReleaseTransferSetV1::empty_for_static_provider_host_owner_v1(),
  1366          );
  1367          Ok(
  1368              SelectedProviderBoundaryExecutionResultForProviderHostOwner {
  1369                  result,
  1370                  selected_output_authority: self.selected_output_authority,
  1371              },
  1372          )
  1373      }
  1374  }
